# Rashi on Zechariah

רש"י על זכריה

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Sefaria vocalized edition (Segments: 366, License: unknown)

Version (En): The Judaica Press complete Tanach with Rashi, translated by A. J. Rosenberg (Segments: 365)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:1:1)


נְבוּאַת זְכַרְיָה סְתוּמָה הִיא מְאֹד, כִּי יֵשׁ בָּהּ מַרְאוֹת דּוֹמוֹת לַחֲלוֹם הַנִּתָּן לְפִתְרוֹן, וְאֵין אָנוּ יְכוֹלִים לַעֲמֹד עַל אֲמִתַּת פִּתְרוֹנוֹ עַד יָבֹא מוֹרֶה צֶדֶק (הושע י:יב). וּלְפִי הַיְּכֹלֶת אֶתֵּן לֵב לְיַשֵּׁב הַמִּקְרָאוֹת אֶחָד אֶחָד מִן הַפִּתְרוֹנוֹת הַדּוֹמוֹת לוֹ, וְאַחַר פִּתְרוֹנוֹת שֶׁל יוֹנָתָן:


**The prophecy** of Zechariah is extremely enigmatic, because it contains visions resembling a dream that requires an interpretation. We cannot ascertain the truth of its interpretation until the teacher of righteousness comes. Nonetheless, I will put my heart to reconciling the verses, one by one, according to the interpretations that resemble it and following the interpretation of *Jonathan*.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:1:2)


**בַּחֹדֶשׁ הַשְּׁמִינִי.** קֹדֶם שֶׁהִתְחִילוּ לַחֲזוֹר וְלִבְנוֹת בַּמְּלָאכָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּעֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה לַחֹדֶשׁ הִתְחִילוּ בָּהּ:


**In the eighth month** Before they commenced resuming the work of the construction, for they commenced it on the twenty-fourth day of the [ninth] month.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:2:1)


**עַל אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם.** שֶׁבְּדוֹר חֻרְבַּן הַבַּיִת:


**with your fathers** who were in the generation of the destruction of the Temple.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:5:1)


**אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם אַיֵּה הֵם.** רְאוּ שֶׁלָּקוּ מַכָּה רָעָה בָּרָעָב בַּחֶרֶב וּבַשֶּׁבִי:


**[As for] your fathers - where are they?** See that they suffered an evil blow, with famine, with the sword, and with pestilence.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:5:2)


**וְהַנְּבִיאִים הַלְעוֹלָם יִחְיוּ.** וְאִם תֹּאמַר: אַף הַנְּבִיאִים אַיֵּה הֵם, הֲרֵי מֵתוּ? אֲנִי מֵשִׁיב לָכֶם: וְכִי הַנְּבִיאִים הָיָה לָהֶם לְעוֹלָם לִחְיוֹת? אַךְ סוֹפָן מוֹכִיחַ כִּי אֱמֶת הָיָה דִּבְרֵיהֶם, שֶׁכָּל דְּבָרַי וְחֻקַּי גְּזֵרוֹת פֻּרְעָנוּתִי אֲשֶׁר צִוִּיתִי אֶת עֲבָדַי הַנְּבִיאִים עֲלֵיכֶם, **הֲלֹא הִשִּׂיגוּ אֲבֹתֵיכֶם.**


**But the prophets - do they live forever?** And if you ask, “the prophets, too where are they? Have they not died?” I [God] will answer you, “Should the prophets have lived forever?” But their end proves that their words were true; for all My words, and the declarations of the decrees of My retribution that I commanded My servants, the prophets, concerning you - did they not overtake your fathers?



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:1)


**רָאִיתִי הַלַּיְלָה וְגוֹ׳.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ מַה שֶּׁפֵּרְשׁוּ בְּאַגָּדַת חֵלֶק (סנהדרין צג.), אַךְ אֵין סֵדֶר הַפָּרָשָׁה וְהַלָּשׁוֹן מִתְיַשֵּׁב בּוֹ:


**I saw tonight** Our Sages explained [this phrase as] they explained [it] in the Aggadah of the chapter entitled “*Helek*” (*Sanh.* 93a), but the sequence of the chapter and the language do not fit it.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:2)


**וְהִנֵּה אִישׁ.** מַלְאָךְ:


**and behold! A man** an angel.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:3)


**עַל סוּס אָדֹם.** רֶמֶז הוּא לִפָּרַע מִכַּשְׂדִּים מִמָּדַי וּפָרַס בַּחֶרֶב וּבַדָּם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר כָּאן ״וְקֶצֶף גָּדוֹל אֲנִי קֹצֵף עַל הָגּוֹיִם״:


**on a red horse** This is an allusion that He would exact retribution upon the Chaldeans, Media, and Persia with the sword and with blood, as He says (verse 15 below): “And I was very wroth with the nations.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:4)


**וְאַחֲרָיו סוּסִים אֲדֻמִּים וְגוֹ׳.** מוּכָנִים לִשְׁלִיחוּתוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם, וְיֵשׁ בָּהֶם מִינֵי צְבָעִים הַרְבֵּה, וְשֶׁבַח הוּא לוֹ. **שְׂרֻקִּים.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַה צֶבַע הוּא, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״קְוָוחִין״:


**and after him were red, etc., horses** prepared for the mission of the Omnipresent. That there were among them [horses of] many sorts of colors was a [means of] praise for him. **black** do not know what color this is. *Jonathan* rendered קְוָחִין.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:8:5)


**בֵּין הַהֲדַסִּים אֲשֶׁר בַּמְּצוּלָה.** בֵּין אִילָנֵי דְהַדַסַּיָּא דִּבְבָבֶל (סנהדרין צג.):


**among the myrtles that were in the pool** Among the myrtle trees of Babylon.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:9:1)


**וָאֹמַר.** לַמַּלְאָךְ אֶחָד אֲשֶׁר הָיָה מַרְאֶה אוֹתִי כָּל זֹאת **מָה אֵלֶּה אֲדֹנִי.** וְהוּא הָרוֹכֵב עַל סוּס אָדֹם (זכריה א:ח):


**And I said** to one angel who showed me all this. **“What are these, my lord?”** He was the one riding on the red horse.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:10:1)


**לְהִתְהַלֵּךְ בָּאָרֶץ.** לִרְאוֹת אֶת שַׁלְוַת הָעַוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**to walk to and fro on the earth** To see the tranquility of the nations.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:12:1)


**וַיַּעַן מַלְאַךְ ה׳.** הוּא הַדּוֹבֵר בִּי:


**And the angel of the Lord replied** The angel that was speaking to me.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:13:1)


**וַיַּעַן ה׳, אֶת הַמַּלְאָךְ הַדּוֹבֵר בִּי דְּבָרִים טוֹבִים.** לֹא שֶׁשָּׁמַע הַנָּבִיא מַה עָנָה, אַךְ מִמַּה שֶּׁאָמַר הַמַּלְאָךְ לוֹ ״קְרָא לֵאמֹר קִנֵּאתִי וְגוֹ׳״, אֲנִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁדְּבָרִים טוֹבִים עָנָהוּ:


**And the Lord answered the angel who was speaking to me, good words** Not that the prophet heard what He answered [the angel], but from what the angel said to him, “Proclaim, saying, ‘I am jealous etc.,’” I know that He answered him good words.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:14:1)


**קִנֵּאתִי לְצִיּוֹן.** ״מִתְפָּרַע אֲנָא פּוּרְעָנוּת דִּין יְרוּשָׁלַיִם מִן הַמְּצִיקִים לָהּ״:


**I am jealous for Jerusalem** I am exacting the retribution [for] the cause of Jerusalem from those who oppress her.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:15:1)


**הַשַּׁאֲנַנִּים.** אֲשֶׁר אֵלּוּ אוֹמְרִים עֲלֵיהֶם, שֶׁהֵם יוֹשְׁבִים וּשְׁקֵטִים:


**that are at ease** Concerning whom these [messengers] say that they sit still and are at rest.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:15:2)


**אֲשֶׁר אֲנִי קָצַפְתִּי.** עַל עַמִּי מְעָט:


**for I was wroth** with My people, a little.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:15:3)


**וְהֵמָּה עָזְרוּ.** עַל הַקֶּצֶף לְרָעָה:


**and they helped to do harm** In addition to the wrath, [they helped] to do harm.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Zechariah 1:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/1:16:1)


**וְקָו יִנָּטֶה.** לָמֹד חוֹמוֹתֶיהָ אָרְכָּהּ וְרָחְבָּהּ:


**and a plumb line shall be stretched out** to measure its wall, its length and breadth.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:2:1)


**אֵלֶּה הַקְּרָנוֹת.** אֵלֶּה הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר בְּאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הַשָּׁמַיִם, אֲשֶׁר נָגְחוּ בְּקַרְנֵיהֶם אֶת יְהוּדָה וִירוּשָׁלִַם, עַד אֲשֶׁר זֵרוּם:


**These are the horns** These are the nations in the four corners of the heavens that gored Judah and Jerusalem with their horns until they scattered them.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:3:1)


**אַרְבָּעָה חָרָשִׁים.** נַגָּרִים הַיּוֹדְעִים לְנַסֵּר אֶת הַקְּרָנוֹת. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סֻכָּה (סוכה נב:) מִי הֵם:


**four craftsmen** Carpenters who know how to saw the horns. And our Rabbis explained in tractate *Sukkah* (52b) who they are.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:1)


**וָאֹמַר מָה.** אֵלֶּה הַקְּרָנוֹת אֲשֶׁר רָאִיתִי בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה:


**And he said, saying, “These are the horns”** that you first saw.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:2)


**אֲשֶׁר זֵרוּ אֶת יְהוּדָה.** וְהִשְׁפִּילוּם, עַד כְּפִי אִישׁ לֹא נָשָׂא רֹאשׁוֹ. וְלֹא נָתְנוּ לְאֶחָד מִבְּנֵי עַמִּי לְהָרִים רֹאשׁ:


**that scattered Judah** and humbled them to the extent that no man lifted his head, and they did not allow anyone of My people to raise his head.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:3)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ אֵלֶּה.** הֶחָרָשִׁים לְהַחֲרִיד אוֹתָן **הַקְּרָנוֹת** וּלְיַדּוֹת אוֹתָן:


**And these came** These craftsmen came to cause those horns to quake and to cast them away.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:4:4)


**לְיַדּוֹת.** לְהַשְׁלִיךְ:


**to cast away** לְיַדּוֹת



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:5:1)


**חֶבֶל מִדָּה.** חוּט הַמִּדָּה לִמְדֹּד אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם כַּמָּה יִהְיֶה אָרְכָּהּ וְרָחְבָּהּ:


**a measuring line** A measuring line to measure Jerusalem, how much its length and its breadth shall be.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:7:1)


**וְהִנֵּה הַמַּלְאָךְ הַדֹּבֵר בִּי יוֹצֵא.** הוּא אֲשֶׁר בְּיָדוֹ חֶבֶל הַמִּדָּה:


**And behold, the angel who was speaking with me was coming forth** He is the one with the measuring line in his hand.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:8:1)


**רוּץ דַּבֵּר אֶל הַנַּעַר.** אַל תֵּצֵא עוֹד לָמֹד, כִּי נִמְלַךְ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּפַמַלְיָא שֶׁלּוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא לָהּ קֶצֶב וְשִׁעוּר (בבא בתרא עה:). וְעַתָּה **רוּץ דַּבֵּר** אֶל זְכַרְיָה **לֵאמֹר פְּרָזוֹת תֵּשֵׁב** – מֵאֵין חוֹמָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְיַשְּׁבוּ בָהּ כָּל הַבָּא, וְתִגְדַּל מִיּוֹם אֶל יוֹם:


**Run, speak to this young man** You shall no longer go forth to measure, for the Holy One, blessed be He, has taken counsel with His heavenly household [and decreed] that [Jerusalem] will not have a limit or a measure. **And now, run and speak to Zechariah: It shall be inhabited like unwalled towns**, without a wall, so that all who come there shall settle therein, and it shall grow from day to day.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:9:1)


**וַאֲנִי אֶהְיֶה לָּהּ.** לְמָגֵן כְּאִלּוּ חוֹמַת אֵשׁ סָבִיב לָהּ, וְגַם בְּתוֹכָהּ תִּשְׁכֹּן שְׁכִינָתִי לִכְבוֹדְכֶם:


**But I will be for it** as a shield, as though a wall of fire surrounds it. Also, My Shechinah shall dwell in its midst for your glory.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:10:1)


**הוֹי הוֹי.** אֵין הוֹי אֶלָּא לְשׁוֹן הַכְרָזָה וְהַזְעָקַת קִבּוּץ, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אַכְלוּ לְמַבְדְּרַיָּא״ – הַכְרִיזוּ, כְּמוֹ **יַרְעֵם מִן שָׁמַיִם** (שמואל ב כב:יד) – ״אַכְלוּ מִן שְׁמַיָּא״:


**Ho, Ho!** “Ho” is only an expression of proclamation and an announcement of assembly. And so did *Jonathan* render it: Announce to the scattered ones. אַכְלוּ means announce, as in (II Sam. 22: 14): “The Lord thundered from heaven,” which *Jonathan* renders: אַכְלוּ מִן שְׁמַיַּא


###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:10:2)


**וְנֻסוּ מֵאֶרֶץ.** צְאוּ גָּלֻיּוֹת עַמִּי מִתּוֹךְ בָּבֶל וְאַשּׁוּר:


**Flee from the land of the north** The exiles of My people, [flee] from within Babylon and Assyria.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:11:1)


**הוֹי צִיּוֹן הִמָּלְטִי.** אַף זֶה לְשׁוֹן הַכְרָזָה: הֵאָסְפוּ עַם גָּלוּת צִיּוֹן וְהִמָּלְטוּ מִתּוֹךְ **בָּבֶל.**


**Ho, Zion! Flee** This too is an expression of announcement: Assemble, O exile of Zion, and flee from the midst of Babylon.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:12:1)


**אַחַר כָּבוֹד.** אַחַר כְּבוֹדְכֶם אֲנִי שָׁלוּחַ לְהַגְדִּיל לָכֶם כָּבוֹד. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה (תנחומא בובר, דברים ד): אַחַר כָּבוֹד – אַחַר שֶׁאֲשַׁלֵּם לְעֵשָׂו כָּבוֹד שֶׁכִּבֵּד אֶת אָבִיו:


**After glory** After your glory I am sent, to magnify your glory. And the Midrash Aggadah (Addendum to *Tan.* Buber, *Devarim* 4) states: After the honor - After I pay Esau for the honor that he bestowed upon his father.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:13:1)


**וְהָיוּ שָׁלָל לְעַבְדֵיהֶם.** לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁהֵם עַתָּה עֲבָדִים לָהֶן:


**and they shall be prey, etc.,** to Israel, who are now slaves to them.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:15:1)


**וְנִלְווּ.** וְנִתְחַבְּרוּ:


**And many nations shall join** shall join.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:16:1)


**וְנָחַל ה׳ אֶת יְהוּדָה.** לְנַחֲלָתוֹ וּלְחֶלְקוֹ:


**And the Lord shall inherit Judah** as His inheritance and His share.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:17:1)


**הַס כָּל בָּשָׂר.** כָּל שְׁאָר הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**Silence all flesh** All the rest of the nations.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 2:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/2:17:2)


**כִּי נֵעוֹר.** לְשׁוֹן הֶעָרָה וְהַקָּצָה (תהלים לה:כג):


**for He is aroused** An expression [denoting] arousal and awakening.



## Chapter 3


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:1:1)


**לְשִׂטְנוֹ.** לְהַשְׂטִינוֹ, עַל שֶׁהָיוּ בָּנָיו נְשׂוּאִים נָשִׁים נָכְרִיּוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא: ״וַיִּמָּצֵא מִבְּנֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים אֲשֶׁר הוֹשִׁיבוּ נָשִׁים נָכְרִיּוֹת מִבְּנֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן יְהוֹצָדָק״ (עזרא י:יח):


**to accuse him** To accuse him because his sons were married to gentile women, as it is written in the Book of Ezra (10:18): “And it was found of the sons of the priests who had taken foreign wives, of the sons of Jeshua the son of Jozadak, etc.”



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:2:1)


**יִגְעַר ה׳ בְּךָ הַשָּׂטָן.** יִגְעַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא בְּךָ אַתָּה הַשָּׂטָן, וְחוֹזֵר וְאוֹמֵר **וְיִגְעַר ה׳ בְּךָ** הוּא **הַבֹּחֵר בִּירוּשָׁלִָם**, שֶׁלֹּא תִּכָּנֵס לְפָנָיו לְקַטְרֵג עַל הַצַּדִּיק הַזֶּה. הֲלֹא רָאוּי הוּא וְזָכָה לְכָךְ שֶׁהֻצַּל **מֵאֵשׁ** הַשְּׂרֵפָה:


**The Lord shall rebuke you, O Satan** The Holy One, blessed be He, shall rebuke you, O you Satan (and then he repeated and said: The Lord shall rebuke you, He who chose Jerusalem *Moharaz Margolioth*); and He Who rebukes you is the One Who chose Jerusalem, that you shall not enter before Him to accuse this righteous man. Is he not fit, and has he not merited this? For he was saved from the consuming fire.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:2:2)


**הֲלֹא זֶה אוּד מֻצָּל מֵאֵשׁ.** מְפֹרָשׁ בְּאַגָּדַת חֵלֶק (סנהדרין צג.) שֶׁהֻשְׁלַךְ עִם אַחְאָב בֶּן קוֹלָיָה וַחֲבֵרוֹ לָאוּר:


**Is this one not a brand plucked from fire?** It is related in the Aggadah of [chapter] *Helek* Helek (*Sanh.* 93a) that he [Joshua] was cast into the fire with Ahab son of Kolaiah and his colleague.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:3:1)


**לָבוּשׁ בְּגָדִים צוֹאִים.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״הֲווֹ לֵיהּ בְּנִין דְּנָסְבִין לְהוֹן נְשִׁין דְּלָא כַשְׁרִין לִכְהוּנְתָא״, וְהָיָה נֶעֱנָשׁ הוּא עַל שֶׁלֹּא מִיחָה בְּיָדָם (סנהדרין צג.):


**was wearing filthy garments** This is to be explained according to the Targum: He had sons who had married women who were unfit [to marry into] the priesthood, and he was punished because he did not interfere with the [sons’ marriages].



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:4:1)


**הָסִירוּ הַבְּגָדִים הַצּוֹאִים מֵעָלָיו.** יַבְדִּילוּ בָּנָיו אֶת נְשׁוֹתֵיהֶם, וְיִמְחוֹל לוֹ:


**“Take the filthy garments off him.”** Let his sons separate from their wives, and he will be forgiven.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:4:2)


**מַחֲלָצוֹת.** חֲלִיפוֹת בְּגָדִים נָאִים, כְּלוֹמַר: זְכֻיּוֹת. אֲבָל לְפִי שֶׁדִּמָּה הֶעָוֹן **לִבְגָדִים צוֹאִים**, דִּמָּה הַזְּכֻיּוֹת **לְמַחֲלָצוֹת** בְּגָדִים נָאִים וּלְבָנִים:


**clean garments** A change of beautiful garments; i.e., merits. Since he compared the iniquity to filthy garments, he compares the merit to clean garments; beautiful, white garments.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:5:1)


**וָאֹמַר.** אֲנִי זְכַרְיָה:


**And I said** I, Zechariah.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:5:2)


**יָשִׂימוּ צָנִיף טָהוֹר וְגוֹ׳.** בִּקַּשְׁתִּי רַחֲמִים עָלָיו:


**“Let them put a pure miter, etc.”** I begged mercy for him.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:6:1)


**וַיָּעַד.** לְשׁוֹן הַתְרָאָה, כְּמוֹ **וְאָעִידָה בָם אֶת הַשָּׁמַיִם וְגוֹ׳** (דברים לא:כח):


**warned** an expression of warning, as in (Deut. 31:28): “And I will warn them before the heaven and the earth.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:1)


**אִם בִּדְרָכַי תֵּלֵךְ.** וְגַם אֲנִי זֹאת אֶעֱשֶׂה לְךָ:


**If you walk in My ways, etc.** Then I, too, will do this for you.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:2)


**אַתָּה תָדִין אֶת בֵּיתִי.** תִּשְׁפּוֹט וְתִהְיֶה פָּקִיד עַל בֵּית מִקְדָּשִׁי:


**you, too, shall judge My house** You shall judge and be the officer over My Temple.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:3)


**וְנָתַתִּי לְךָ מַהְלְכִים.** כְּתַרְגּוּמוֹ: ״וּבַאֲחָיוּת מֵיתַיָּא אֲחַיֵּינָךְ וְאֶתֵּן לָךְ רַגְלִין דִּמְהַלְּכִין בֵּין שְׂרָפַיָּא הָאִלֵּין״. וּלְפִי פְשׁוּטוֹ: מְבַשְּׂרוֹ עַל בָּנָיו שֶׁיִּזְכּוּ לֶעָתִיד:


**and I will give you free access** According to the Targum: and when the dead will be resurrected, I will resurrect you; and I will give you walkers who walk among these seraphim. According to its simple meaning, he brings him tidings that his sons will be meritorious in the future.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:7:4)


**הָעוֹמְדִים.** שְׂרָפִים וּמַלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם יְשִׁיבָה (שמות רבה כה):


**who stand by** Seraphim and ministering angels, who never sit.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:8:1)


**אַתָּה וְרֵעֶיךָ.** חֲנַנְיָה מִישָׁאֵל וַעֲזַרְיָה הָיוּ (סנהדרין צג.):


**you and your companions** They were Hananiah, Mishael, and Azariah.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:8:2)


**אַנְשֵׁי מוֹפֵת.** ״גֻּבְרִין כָּשְׁרִין לְמֵיעֲבַד לְהוֹן נִיסִּין״, שֶׁאַף לָהֶם נַעֲשָׂה נֵס (סנהדרין צג.)׃


**for they are men worthy of a miracle** [*Jonathan* renders:] Men worthy to have miracles performed for them, for a miracle was performed for them, too.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:8:3)


**כִּי הִנְנִי מֵבִיא אֶת עַבְדִּי צֶמַח.** כִּי עַתָּה זְרֻבָּבֶל פַּחַת יְהוּדָה קָטָן בַּחֲצַר הַמֶּלֶךְ, אֲנִי מַצְמִיחַ לוֹ גְּדֻלָּה וְשֵׁם. נְתַתִּיו לְחֵן בְּעֵינֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ לְמַלֹּאות שְׁאֵלָתוֹ בְּבִנְיַן הַבַּיִת וְהָעִיר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּעֶזְרָא דִּבְרֵי נְחֶמְיָה בֶּן חֲכַלְיָה, וְהוּא זְרֻבָּבֶל, כְּדַאֲמְרִינָן בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (סנהדרין לח.):


**for, behold! I bring My servant, the Shoot** For now Zerubbabel, the governor of Judah, is insignificant in the king’s court, but I will make his greatness burgeon. I will also give him favor in the eyes of the king, so that he will grant [Zerubbabel’s] request for the building of the Temple and the city, as explained in Nehemiah (1:1): “The words of Nehemiah, the son of Hachaliah.” Nehemiah was identical with Zerubbabel, as we say in *Sanhedrin* (38a).



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:1)


**כִּי הִנֵּה הָאֶבֶן.** יְסוֹד הַבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר יְסַדְתֶּם בִּימֵי כּוֹרֶשׁ וּבָטְלוּ עַל יְדֵיכֶם, וְאוֹתוֹ הַיְּסוֹד וְעֹבִי הַחוֹמָה הָיָה מְעַט בְּעֵינֵיכֶם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְפֹרָשׁ בְּעֶזְרָא (עזרא ג:יב) וּבִנְבוּאַת חַגַּי (חגי ב:ג):


**For, behold the stone** The foundation of the house that you laid in the days of Cyrus - they stopped you, and that foundation and the thickness of the wall appeared little in your sight, as explained in Ezra (3:12f.) and in the prophecy of Haggai (2:2f.).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:2)


**עַל אֶבֶן אַחַת שִׁבְעָה עֵינָיִם.** סוֹפוֹ לְהַרְחִיב עַל אֶחָד **שִׁבְעָה**, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״עַל אַבְנָא חֲדָא שִׁבְעָה עֵינִין חַזְיָן לָהּ״, וּלְשׁוֹן **עֵינַיִם** לְשׁוֹן מַרְאוֹת. וְלִי נִרְאֶה: **שִׁבְעָה עֵינַיִם** עַל שֵׁם עֵינֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁבְּעֵינָיו וּבְלִבּוֹ לְהוֹסִיף עָלָיו **עַל** אֶחָד **שִׁבְעָה**, לְפִי שֶׁדֻּגְמָתוֹ בִּנְבוּאָה זוֹ ״וְרָאוּ אֶת הָאֶבֶן הַבְּדִיל בְּיַד זְרֻבָּבֶל שִׁבְעָה אֵלֶּה עֵינֵי ה׳ הֵמָּה מְשׁוֹטְטִים וְגוֹ׳״ (זכריה ד:י). אֶלָּא לִמֵּד, שֶׁנָּתַן עֵינָיו לְהַרְבּוֹת כְּבוֹד הַבַּיִת בְּבִנְיָנוֹ:


**Seven eyes are directed to one stone** It will eventually be widened seven times as much. And so did *Jonathan* render: On one stone, seven eyes see it. The expression of עֵינַיִם is an expression of appearances. It appears to me that the seven eyes are because of the eyes of the Holy One, blessed be He in Whose eyes and heart it is [planned] to add seven times as much because of the prophecy similar to this one (4:10): “And they shall see the plummet in the hand of Zerubbabel these seven times; the eyes of the Lord they are roving to and fro throughout the land.” This is but to say that He directed His eyes to increase the glory of the Temple in its building.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:3)


**הִנְנִי מְפַתֵּחַ פְּתוּחָה.** מַתִּיר קִשּׁוּרֶיהָ, מֵפִיר עֲצַת הָאוֹיְבִים אֲשֶׁר כָּתְבוּ שִׂטְנָה לְבַטֵּל הַמְּלָאכָה (עזרא ד:ו):


**Behold! I untie its knots** I untie its knots I thwart the plot of the enemies who wrote an accusation to stop the work.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:4)


**וּמַשְׁתִּי.** וַהֲסִירוֹתִי:


**and I will remove** Heb. ומשתי.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 3:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/3:9:5)


**בְּיוֹם אֶחָד.** לֹא יָדַעְתִּי אֵיזֶה יוֹם. [תוספת: בְּיוֹם אֲשֶׁר הִתְחִילוּ לְהוֹסִיף עַל הַבִּנְיָן יָמִישׁ **עֲוֺן הָאָרֶץ** וְתִהְיֶה בְּרָכָה בְּפֵירוֹתָיו, כִּי עַכְשָׁיו מְאֵירָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בִּנְבוּאַת חַגַּי (חגי ב:טז)]:


**in one day** I do not know what day.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:2:1)


**וְגֻלָּה עַל רֹאשָׁהּ.** כְּמוֹ (יהושע טו:יט) גֻּלּוֹת עִלִּית, לְשׁוֹן מַעְיָן, כְּמִין סֵפֶל גָּדוֹל עָגוֹל:


**with its oil - bowl on top of it** as in (Josh. 15:19): “The upper springs”. This is an expression for a spring, [hence] a sort of large round bowl.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:2:2)


**וְשִׁבְעָה נֵרֹתֶיהָ.** כְּמִין בָּזִיכִין שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין הַשֶּׁמֶן וְהַפְּתִילָה לְתוֹכָהּ:


**and its seven lamps** A type of vessel into which oil and wicks are inserted.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:2:3)


**שִׁבְעָה וְשִׁבְעָה מוּצָקוֹת.** לְכָל נֵר וָנֵר בָּאִין **שִׁבְעָה** צִנּוֹרוֹת קְטַנִּים, שֶׁהַשֶּׁמֶן זָב מִן **הַגֻּלָּה** דֶּרֶךְ אוֹתָן **מוּצָקוֹת** לְכָל נֵר וָנֵר:


**seven tubes each** Seven small tubes come to every lamp, for the oil flows from the bowl through those tubes into each lamp.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:3:1)


**וּשְׁנַיִם זֵיתִים עָלֶיהָ.** אֶצְלָהּ שְׁנֵי אִילָנוֹת שֶׁהַזֵּיתִים גְּדֵלִים בָּהֶם:


**And [there were] two olive trees near it** Beside it were two trees upon which olives were growing.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:3:2)


**אֶחָד מִימִין הַגֻּלָּה וְאֶחָד מִשְּׂמֹאלָהּ וְגוֹ׳.** וְכָאן לֹא פֵּרַשׁ עַל שְׁנֵי צַנְתְּרוֹת זָהָב הָאֲמוּרוֹת לְמַטָּה בַּפָּרָשָׁה, וְהֵם כְּמִין עֲרֵבוֹת וַעֲדָשִׁים שֶׁל בֵּית הַבַּד, שֶׁהֵם עוֹמְדִים אֵצֶל הַזֵּיתִים, וְהַזֵּיתִים נֶחְבָּטִים מֵאֲלֵיהֶם לְתוֹךְ הַצַּנְתְּרוֹת וּמִתְחַמְּמוֹת שָׁם כְּבַמַּעֲטָן וְנֶעֱצָרִים שָׁם בְּבֵית הַבַּד, וְהַשֶּׁמֶן נוֹפֵל לְתוֹךְ הַצַּנְתְּרוֹת וּמֵהַצַּנְתְּרוֹת לְתוֹךְ הַגֻּלָּה, וּמֵהַגֻּלָּה אֶל הַמּוּצָקוֹת, וּמֵהַמּוּצָקוֹת אֶל הַנֵּרוֹת. וְהַמּוּצָקוֹת וְהַנֵּרוֹת אַרְבָּעִים וְתֵשַׁע הָיוּ, רֶמֶז לָאוֹר שֶׁלֶּעָתִיד לָבֹא, אוֹר הַחַמָּה יִהְיֶה שִׁבְעָתַיִם כְּאוֹר שֶׁל שִׁבְעַת יָמִים (ישעיה ל:כו), אַרְבָּעִים וְתֵשַׁע עַל אוֹר שֶׁל יוֹם בְּרֵאשִׁית:


**one on the right of the bowl, one on the its left, etc.** Here [the prophet] does not explain about the two golden vats mentioned below in the chapter, which are the sorts of bowls or vats of the oil press. [These vats] stand beside the olive trees. The olives beat themselves into the vats and are heated there as [if] in a vat or pit where olives are generally packed. There they are pressed in the oil press, and the oil falls into the vats, and from the vats into the bowl, and from the bowl into the tubes, and from the tubes into the lamps. The tubes and the lamps number forty-nine, an allusion to the light, for in the future the light of the sun will be sevenfold the light of the seven days forty-nine times the light of a day of Creation.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:4:1)


**מָה אֵלֶּה אֲדֹנִי.** מַה זֶּה שֶׁהַזֵּיתִים נִמְסָקִים מֵאֲלֵיהֶם, וְהַשֶּׁמֶן בָּא אֶל הַנֵּרוֹת מֵאֵלָיו:


**“What are these, my Lord?”** What is this, that the olive trees are picked by themselves, and the oil comes into the lamps by itself?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:6:1)


**זֶה דְּבַר ה׳ אֶל זְרֻבָּבֶל.** זֶה סִימָן לְךָ לְהַבְטִיחַ אֶת זְרֻבָּבֶל, כְּשֵׁם שֶׁהַזֵּיתִים וְהַשֶּׁמֶן הַזֶּה נִגְמָר מֵאֵלָיו לְכָל דְּבָרָיו, כָּךְ **לֹא בְחַיִל וְלֹא בְכֹחַ** שֶׁבָּכֶם תַּעֲשׂוּ אֶת בִּנְיַן בֵּיתִי:


**“This is the word of the Lord to Zerubbabel”** This is a sign for you to promise Zerubbabel that just as the olives and this oil are finished by themselves in all respects, so will you not build My house with your [own] power or with your [own] strength.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:6:2)


**כִּי אִם בְּרוּחִי.** אֲנִי אֶתֵּן רוּחִי עַל דָּרְיָוֶשׁ וְיַנִּיחַ לָכֶם (ספרים אחרים: וִיצַוֶּה לָכֶם) לִבְנוֹת וְלַעֲשׂוֹת כָּל צָרְכֵי יְצִיאַת הַבִּנְיָן מִשֶּׁלּוֹ, וְלַעֲזֹר אֶתְכֶם בְּחִטִּין וְיַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן וְעֵצִים, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּעֶזְרָא (עזרא ו:ח-ט). וְלֹא תִצְטָרְכוּ לְעֶזְרַת אָדָם:


**but by My spirit** I will place My spirit upon Darius, and he will command you to build and to pay all the building expenses from his [treasury]; and [he will] help you with wheat, wine, oil, and wood, as is explained in Ezra (6, 7): They required no aid from any man.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:7:1)


**מִי אַתָּה הַר הַגָּדוֹל.** שָׂרֵי עֵבֶר הַנָּהָר ״תַּתְּנַי פַּחַת עֵבֶר נַהֲרָה וּשְׁתַר בּוֹזְנַי וּכְנָוָתְהוֹן״ (עזרא ה:ג), שֶׁבִּטַּלְתֶּם הַמְּלָאכָה עַד כֹּה; מֵעַתָּה תִּהְיֶה **לִפְנֵי זְרֻבָּבֶל לְמִישׁוֹר**, אֵין לָכֶם שְׂרָרָה וּגְדֻלָּה עוֹד עָלָיו:


**Who are you, O great mountain?** You, the princes of the other side of the river Tattenai, the governor of the other side of the river; Shethar Bozenai, and their companions (Ezra 6:6), who have stopped the work until now - from now on you shall be before Zerubbabel as a plain; you have no more ruling power or superiority over him.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:7:2)


**וְהוֹצִיא אֶת הָאֶבֶן הָרֹאשָׁה.** הָאַדְרִיכָל יִקַּח אֶת אֶבֶן הַבְּדִיל בְּיָדוֹ (זכריה ד:י) לִהְיוֹת אַדְרִיכָל בְּרֹאשׁ הַבּוֹנִים, וְהֵם יִבְנוּ הַכֹּל כְּפִי דְּבָרָיו אֲשֶׁר יְצַוֶּה בְּבִנְיָן נָאֶה וּמְפֹאָר:


**He will bring out the stone of the main architect** The main architect will take the plummet in his hand to be the main architect at the head of the builders, and they will build everything according to his words [everything] that he will order concerning a beautiful and glorious building.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:7:3)


**תְּשׁוּאוֹת חֵן חֵן.** יִהְיֶה לָהּ לְאוֹתָהּ הָאֶבֶן שֶׁהַכֹּל יֹאמְרוּ מַה נָּאֶה בִּנְיָן זֶה הֶעָשׂוּי עַל יְדֵי אֶבֶן הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת הַזֹּאת, תְּשׁוּאוֹת חֵן כְּמוֹ תְּשׁוּאוֹת נוֹי כְּמוֹ ״תְּשׁוּאוֹת מְלֵאָה עִיר הוֹמִיָּה״ (ישעיהו כב:ב), ״קוֹל שָׁאוֹן״ (ישעיהו סו:ו) כֻּלָּם לְשׁוֹן הַשְׁמָעַת קוֹל הֵם:


**with shouts of grace, grace to it** To that stone, for everyone will say, “How beautiful is this building that was made with this plummet.” [The expression] “shouts of grace” is as (Job 39:7) “the shouts of a driver,” and (Isa. 66:6) “a sound of stirring” both of which are expressions of making a voice heard.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:9:1)


**יְדֵי זְרֻבָּבֶל יִסְּדוּ הַבַּיִת הַזֶּה.** מִתְּחִלָּה וְעַד סוֹף בִּימֵי כּוֹרֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן (עזרא ג:י):


**Zerubbabel’s hands founded this house** from beginning to end, in the days of Cyrus I.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:9:2)


**וִידָיו תְּבַצַּעְנָה.** אוֹתוֹ עַתָּה, תְּבַצַּעְנָה לְשׁוֹן גְּמַר הוּא, כְּמוֹ ״כִּי יְבַצַּע ה׳ כָּל מַעֲשֵׂהוּ״ (ישעיה י:יב):


**and his hands shall complete** it now. The word תְּבַצַעְנָה is an expression of completion, as in (Isa. 10: 12) “when the Lord completes all His work.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:10:1)


**כִּי מִי בַז לְיוֹם.** שֶׁיֻּסַּד הַבַּיִת אֲשֶׁר הֻקְטַן בְּעֵינֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (עזרא ג:יב) ״וְרַבִּים מֵהַכֹּהֲנִים אֲשֶׁר רָאוּ הַבַּיִת הָרִאשׁוֹן בְּיָסְדוֹ זֶה הַבַּיִת בְּעֵינֵיהֶם בֹּכִים בְּקוֹל גָּדוֹל״, וּבִנְבוּאַת חַגַּי (חגי ב:ג) ״הֲלֹא כְמוֹהוּ כְּאַיִן בְּעֵינֵיהֶם״. **וְשָׂמְחוּ** עַתָּה כְּשֶׁיִּרְאוּ **אֶת הָאֶבֶן הַבְּדִיל** הַתְּלוּיָה בְּחוּט הַמִּשְׁקֹלֶת **בְּיַד** אַדְרִיכַל רֹאשׁ הַבּוֹנִים, שֶׁמְּכַוֵּן בָּהּ רֹאשׁ הַפִּנָּה. וְזוֹ תִּהְיֶה **בְּיַד זְרֻבָּבֶל.**


**For, whoever despised the day** when the Temple was founded which was small in their eyes, as is stated in Ezra (3:12): “But many of priests, etc., who had seen, etc., upon its foundation, wept aloud when they beheld this temple;” and in Haggai (2:3): “Is it not as nothing in your eyes?” - will rejoice now when they see the plummet suspended from the plumb line in the hand of the architect, the head of the builders, with which he directs a line to the corner, and this will be in the hand of Zerubbabel.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:10:2)


**שִׁבְעָה אֵלֶּה.** עַל אֶחָד **שִׁבְעָה** בַּיְּסוֹד הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁבִּימֵי כּוֹרֶשׁ, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״שִׁבְעָה נִדְבָּכִין כְּאִלֵּין״:


**these, sevenfold** Seven times the first foundation in the days of Cyrus. So did *Jonathan* render: Seven rows of stones like these.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:10:3)


**עֵינֵי ה׳ הֵמָּה מְשׁוֹטְטִים.** וְלוֹ נִרְאֶה לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן, וְהוּא רָאָה אֶת זְרֻבָּבֶל זֶה הָגוּן לַדָּבָר:


**the eyes of the Lord are roving to and fro** And it appeals to Him to do so; and He saw this Zerubbabel suited for the matter.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:11:1)


**מַה שְּׁנֵי הַזֵּיתִים.** מָה רֶמֶז הֵם, וּמָה רֶמֶז **שְׁנֵי שִׁבֲּלֵי זֵיתִים** טְרוֹקָ״א בְּלַעַ״ז, קְבוּצוֹת זֵיתִים בְּעָנָף כְּעֵין שִׁבֹּלֶת:


**What are these two olive trees?** What do they symbolize, and what do the two olive branches (*troches* in Old French) symbolize? [They are] a cluster of olives on a branch, as if a type of ear of grain.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:12:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בְּיַד שְׁנֵי צַנְתְּרוֹת.** כְּלוֹמַר: אֵצֶל **שְׁנֵי הַצַּנְתְּרוֹת**, כְּמוֹ ״רְאוּ חֶלְקַת יוֹאָב אֶל יָדִי״ (שמואל ב יד:ל):


**beside the two golden vats** i.e., beside the two golden vats, as in (II Sam. 14:30): “See, Joab’s field is near mine.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:12:2)


**הַמְּרִיקִים מֵעֲלֵיהֶם.** אֶת הַשֶּׁמֶן הַזֶּה הַצָּלוּל וְטוֹב לְהָאִיר כְּזָהָב, כְּמוֹ (אִיּוֹב לז:כב) ״מִצָּפוֹן זָהָב יֶאֱתֶה״:


**that empty out the gold[en oil] from themselves** This oil that is clear and good to illuminate like gold, as in (Job 37:22): “From the north comes gold.”



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:14:1)


**שְׁנֵי בְנֵי הַיִּצְהָר.** קֶרֶן כְּהֻנָּה וּמַלְכוּת, הַנִּמְשָׁחִים בְּשֶׁמֶן הַמִּשְׁחָה:


**two anointed ones** The horns of the priesthood and the kingdom who are anointed with the anointing oil.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:14:2)


**הָעוֹמְדִים עַל אֲדוֹן כָּל הָאָרֶץ.** לְבַקֵּשׁ מִלְּפָנָיו לְהַחֲזִיר אֶת גְּדֻלָּתָם:


**who stand before the Lord of all the earth** to supplicate Him to return their greatness.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 4:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/4:14:3)


**שְׁנֵי בְנֵי הַיִּצְהָר.** יֵצֶר טוֹב וְיֵצֶר הָרָע מִתְהַפֵּךְ לְטוֹב בִּזְכוּת הַתּוֹרָה:


**the two anointed ones** The good inclination and the evil inclination, which is converted to good in the merit of the Torah.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:1:1)


**מְגִלָּה עָפָה.** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ כְּפוּלָה וְאָמְרוּ שֶׁהַתּוֹרָה כְּתוּבָה בָּהּ וְלָמְדוּ מִכָּאן שֶׁהָעוֹלָם אֶחָד מִשְּׁלֹשֶׁת אֲלָפִים וּמָאתַיִם בַּתּוֹרָה כִּדְאִיתָא בְּעֵירוּבִין (עירובין כא.) וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם ״מְגִלְּתָא פָּרְחָא פּוֹרַחַת בָּאֲוִיר״, וּלְפִי פְשׁוּטוֹ שֶׁל פָּרָשָׁה מְגִלַּת פֻּרְעָנוּת הָיְתָה וְהוּא שֶׁרָאָה (יחזקאל ב:י) ״וְכָתוּב אֵלֶיהָ קִינִים וָהֶגֶה וָהִי״:


**a flying scroll** Our Sages explained [it] as “double,” and they said that the Torah was written on it. They deduced from here that the world is 1/3,200 of the Torah, as stated in Eruvin (21a). And Jonathan rendered: a flying scroll; i.e., flying in the air. According to the simple meaning of the chapter, it was a scroll of retribution; that was what Ezekiel (2:10) envisioned: “And in it was written lamentations and mourning and woe.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:1)


**זֹאת הָאָלָה.** זֹאת שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהּ פֻּרְעָנוּת נִקְמַת שְׁבוּעוֹת שֶׁקֶר:


**This is the curse** This is [the scroll] in which is written the retribution, the revenge for a false oath.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:2)


**הַיּוֹצֵאת.** עַתָּה מִבֵּית קָדְשֵׁי הַקֳּדָשִׁים מִלִּפְנֵי הַשְּׁכִינָה לְהִתְהַלֵּךְ **עַל פְּנֵי כָל הָאָרֶץ** וְזֶהוּ אָרְכָּהּ עֶשְׂרִים וְרָחְבָּהּ עֶשֶׂר (זכריה ה:ב) כְּמִדַּת פֶּתַח הָעֲזָרָה וְהַהֵיכָל (מלכים א ו:ג) אֲשֶׁר יָצְאָת דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם:


[The scroll] **is now coming forth** from the Holy of Holies, from before the Shechinah, to walk to and fro upon the face of the entire land; this is the meaning of “its length is twenty, and its width is ten” - as the measure of the entrance to the Temple court and the Temple, through which it shall emerge.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:3)


**כִּי כָל הַגַּנָּב.** עַד עַכְשָׁיו:


**for whoever stole** until now.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:3:4)


**מִזֶּה כָּמוֹהָ נִקָּה.** מִזֶּה הַפֻּרְעָנוּת כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא מְפֹרָשׁ בָּהּ **נִקָּה** וְלֹא נִשְׁתַּלֵּם פֻּרְעָנוּת יְחִידִים הָעוֹבְרִים עַל הַתּוֹרָה, אֶלָּא הִמְתִּין עַד שֶׁנִּתְמַלֵּאת סְאַת כֻּלָּם, וְלָקוּ כֻּלָּם כְּאֶחָד בַּחֻרְבָּן וּבַגּוֹלָה. וְכֵן **כָּל הַנִּשְׁבָּע** לַשֶּׁקֶר עַד הֵנָּה **מִזֶּה** הַפֻּרְעָנוּת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהַמְּגִלָּה הַזֹּאת מַגֶּדֶת: **נִקָּה** וְלֹא נִשְׁתַּלֵּם. אֲבָל מִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ **הוֹצֵאתִיהָ** לָשׁוּט בָּאָרֶץ וְלִנְקֹם מִן הַגַּנָּבִים וְהַנִּשְׁבָּעִים לַשֶּׁקֶר מֵעַכְשָׁיו, **וּבָא אֶל בֵּית הַגַּנָּב וְגוֹ׳**:


**was cleared from such as this** He was cleared from this retribution, as it delineated on it [i.e., the scroll]. The retribution of individuals who transgressed the Torah was not meted out, but He waited until the measure of all of them was filled, and they all suffered at once with the destruction and the exile. Similarly, everyone who heretofore swore falsely, was cleared of the retribution as delineated in this scroll and was not requited, but from now on... **I have brought it forth** to walk to and fro in the land and to wreak vengeance upon the thieves and the swearers of falsehoods from now on; and it shall come into the house of the thief, etc.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:5:1)


**שָׂא נָא עֵינֶיךָ וּרְאֵה.** עוֹד יוֹצֵאת אַחֶרֶת שֶׁתֵּצֵא מִבֵּית קֹדֶשׁ הַקֳּדָשִׁים:


**Now lift up your eyes and see** Yet another is coming forth, that will come out from the Holy of Holies.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:6:1)


**וָאֹמַר מַה הִיא.** הַיּוֹצֵאת שֶׁתֹּאמַר אֵלַי לִרְאוֹת:


**And I said, “What is it?”** that is coming forth, that you tell me to see?


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:6:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר זֹאת הָאֵיפָה.** שֶׁתֵּרָאֶה יוֹצֵאת כְּמִין אֵיפָה שֶׁמּוֹדְדִין בָּהּ:


**And he said, “This is the ephah”** For you will see a sort of ephah with which they measure coming forth.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:6:3)


**וַיֹּאמֶר זֹאת עֵינָם בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ.** וּלְאַחַר שֶׁרְאִיתִיהָ אָמַר: זֹאת הִיא מִדָּה שֶׁלָּקוּ בָּהּ אוֹתָם שֶׁעֵינֵיהֶם בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ לִגְזֹל וְלַעֲשֹׁק, ״לְהַקְטִין אֵיפָה וּלְהַגְדִּיל שֶׁקֶל״ (עמוס ח:ה), וְנִמְדַּד לָהֶם סְאָה בִּסְאָה:


**And he said, “This is [the punishment of those] whose eye [gazes] over the entire land”** And, after I saw it, he said, “This is the measure by which punishment was meted out to those whose eyes were over the entire land, to rob and to oppress, to make the ephah smaller and to make the shekel larger; and punishment was meted out to them, a measure for a measure.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:7:1)


**וְהִנֵּה כִּכַּר עוֹפֶרֶת נִשֵּׂאת.** רָאִיתִי שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ לָאֲוִיר מִשְׁקַל כִּכַּר עוֹפֶרֶ,ת וְרָאִיתִי עוֹד **זֹאת אִשָּׁה אַחַת יוֹשֶׁבֶת בְּתוֹךְ הָאֵיפָה**:


**And behold! A talent of lead was being lifted** I saw that a weight [made] of a talent of lead was being lifted off the earth into the air, and I saw further that this one woman was sitting in the midst of the ephah.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:8:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר זֹאת הָרִשְׁעָה.** הָאִשָּׁה זֹאת שֶׁאַתָּה רוֹאֶה בְּתוֹךְ הָאֵיפָ,ה הִיא מִדַּת דֶּרֶךְ הָרִשְׁעָה אֲשֶׁר יִנְהֲגוּ בָּהּ הָרְשָׁעִים, וְהִנֵּה עַתָּה נְתוּנִים בְּתוֹכָהּ לִלְקוֹת בְּתוֹךְ הַסְּאָה עַצְמָהּ שֶׁמָּדְדוּ בָּהּ, מִדָּה בְּמִדָּה:


**And he said, “This is Wickedness.”** This woman that you see in the midst of the ephah is the character of the way of wickedness, [the way] in which the wicked deport themselves. And behold, now they are placed in its midst to be punished in the midst of that very measure that they meted out, a measure for a measure.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:8:2)


**וַיַּשְׁלֵךְ אֹתָהּ.** עַד עַתָּה הָיְתָה יוֹשֶׁבֶת, וְהוּא הִשְׁלִיכָהּ וְחָבְטָהּ לְתוֹכָהּ, רֶמֶז לְיִסּוּרִים וְלָעֳנָשִׁים:


**And he cast her** Heretofore she had been sitting, but he cast her and knocked her into its midst, symbolizing torments and punishments.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:8:3)


**וַיַּשְׁלֵךְ אֶת אֶבֶן הָעוֹפֶרֶת אֶל פִּיהָ.** לְשַׁקְּעָהּ, לְמַעַן לֹא יִגְדְּלוּ עוֹד, וְלֹא יִשָּׁמַע קוֹלָן לְשַׁדֵּד עֲנִיִּים וְאֶבְיוֹנִים:


**and he cast the lead weight into her mouth** to weigh her down so that they achieve no more greatness and their voice is no longer heard robbing the poor and needy.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:9:1)


**שְׁתַּיִם נָשִׁים יוֹצְאוֹת.** מִן הַבַּיִת:


**two women were coming forth** from the house.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:9:2)


**הַחֲסִידָה.** דַּיָּה לְבָנָה וולטויא״ר בְּלַעַ״ז (חולין סג.)׃


**the stork** the white dayyah (Hullin 63a), voltur in Old French.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:11:1)


**וְהוּכַן וְהֻנִּיחָה שָׁם.** וְהוּכַן הַבַּיִת וְהֻנְּחָה הָאֵיפָה עִם הָרְשָׁעִים שֶׁבְּתוֹכָהּ:


**and it will be prepared, and they shall place it there** And the house shall be prepared, and the ephah shall be placed there with the wicked that are within it.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:11:2)


**שָׁם.** בְּאוֹתוֹ הַבַּיִת:


**there** In that house.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 5:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/5:11:3)


**עַל מְכוּנָתָהּ.** עַל קְבִיעוּתָהּ וְעַל בְּסִיסָהּ. כָּאן רָמַז לוֹ, שֶׁבַּעֲוֹנָם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁמָּדְדוּ בִּסְאַת עֲבֵרוֹת וָרֶשַׁע, לָקוּ בְּאוֹתָהּ מִדָּה. וּבָאוּ שְׁתֵּי אֻמּוֹת שֶׁמָּלְכוּ כְּאַחַת, הֵם בָּבֶל וְכַשְׂדִים, חֵיל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר, וְהִגְלוּם לְבָבֶל; וְשָׁם עָמְדוּ קְבוּעִים עַל מְכוֹן בָּסִיס הַהֻקְבַּע לָהֶם, וְהוּא לְפִי מְלֹאות שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה עַד עַתָּה (ירמיה כט:י), שֶׁחָזְרוּ. וְנוֹטֶה פֵּרוּשׁ זֶה אַחַר יוֹנָתָן בְּמִקְצָת. וְכַמָּה דְּרָכִים פּוֹתְרִים בָּהּ, וְלֹא נִתְיַשְּׁבוּ לִי. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ וְלָמְדוּ מִכָּאן, שֶׁנִּמְסַר לָהֶם יֵצֶר הָרַע לְאַנְשֵׁי כְּנֶסֶת הַגְּדוֹלָה, וְשַׁדְיוּהּ לְדוּדָא דְּאַבְרָא וְחַפְיוּהוּ בְּאַבְרָא מִשּׁוּם דְּשָׁאִיב קָלֵיהּ, כִּדְאִיתָא בְּסַנְהֶדְרִין (סנהדרין סד.) וּבְמַסֶּכֶת יוֹמָא (יומא סט:). וְשֶׁפָּתַרְתִּי מַלְכוּת נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר בְּכֶפֶל שְׁתֵּי אֻמּוֹת – רָאִיתִי בְּמִדְרַשׁ תִּילִּים, שֶׁאַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת הַלָּלוּ שֶׁשִּׁעְבְּדוּ בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, כְּפוּלוֹת הָיוּ: בָּבֶל וְכַשְׂדִים, מָדַי וּפָרַס, אַנְטִיוֹכוּס וּמוֹקְדוֹן, אֱדוֹם וְיִשְׁמָעֵאל. וְעַל כָּכָה יִסֵּד הַקָּלִירִי: רְבִיעִית הִיא שְׁמִינִית, עַל אֱדוֹם:


**on its base** On its permanence and on its base. Here He hinted [to Zechariah] that, because of the iniquity of Israel, who measured out [so to speak] with the measure of sins and wickedness, they were punished with the same measure. And there came two nations that ruled together; they were Babylon and the Chaldeans - the army of Nebuchadnezzar - and exiled [Israel] to Babylon where [Israel] stayed fixed on a base, a base that was established for them. That is according to the completion of the seventy years until now, when they returned. This interpretation leans partially toward that of Jonathan. This [phrase] is interpreted in many [other] ways, but they are not satisfactory to me. Our Sages, however, explained and learned from here that the evil inclination was given into the hands of the men of the Great Assembly, and they cast it into a leaden cauldron and covered it with lead, because lead absorbs the sound, as is stated in tractate Sanhedrin (24a) and in tractate Yoma (69b). My interpretation of the kingdom of Nebuchadnezzar as doubled - composed of two nations - I saw in Midrash Psalms (6:2, 18: 10), that the following four kingdoms that subjugated Israel were double: Babylon and the Chaldeans, Media and Persia, Greece and Macedon, Edom and Ishmael, and for this reason the Kalir composed (in Piyutim for Shemini Azereth), “It is the fourth; it is the eighth,” concerning Edom.



## Chapter 6


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:1:1)


**וְהֶהָרִים הָרֵי נְחֹשֶׁת.** סִימָן עַל חֹזֶק מַלְכֻיּוֹת הַלָּלוּ הַבָּא לָהֶם **מִבֵּין שְׁנֵי הֶהָרִים** שֶׁהוּא קָשֶׁה וְאַמִּיץ, שֶׁאֵלּוּ **אַרְבַּע מַרְכָּבוֹת** שְׁלוּחֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לָתֵת מֶמְשֶׁלֶת לְאַרְבַּע מַלְכֻיּוֹת: בָּבֶל וּמָדַי יָוָן וֶאֱדוֹם:


**and the mountains were mountains of copper** A sign of the strength of these kingdoms, which came to them from between the two mountains, which are hard and strong, as these four chariots are messengers of the Holy one, blessed be He, to give ruling power to the four kingdoms of Babylon, Media, Greece, and Edom.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:2:1)


**סוּסִים אֲדֻמִּים.** הֵם שֶׁהִרְכִּיבוּ אֶת בָּבֶל, שֶׁהִיא אֲדֻמָּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב ״אַנְתְּ הוּא רֵאשָׁה דִּי דַהֲבָא״ (דניאל ב:לח):


**red horses** They were the ones that caused Babylon to mount, for [Babylon] is red, as it is written (Dan. 2:38): “You are the head of gold” [gold having a red tinge].


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:2:2)


**שְׁחוֹרִים.** לְהַרְכִּיב אֶת מָדַי שֶׁהִשְׁחִירָה אֶת פְּנֵיהֶם שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּימֵי הָמָן (מגילה יא.):


**black** [Horses,] to cause Media to mount, for they blackened the faces of Israel in the days of Haman.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:3:1)


**לְבָנִים.** לְהַרְכִּיב אֶת פָּרַס שֶׁבָּנָה אֶת הַבַּיִת (אבות דרבי נתן נוסחא ב כה,ב):


**white** To cause Persia to mount, and Persia built the Temple.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:3:2)


**בְּרֻדִּים.** הֵמָּה מְנֻמָּרִים, לְהַרְכִּיב אֶת יָוָן, שֶׁעִנּוּ אֶת יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמִינֵי גְּזֵרוֹת מְשֻׁנּוֹת זוֹ מִזֶּה (ויקרא רבה יג:ה):


**spotted** They are spotted. [Their function is] to cause Greece to mount, [as Greece] oppressed Israel with various kinds of decrees


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:3:3)


**אֲמֻצִּים.** לְהַרְכִּיב יִשְׁמָעֵאל (ספרים אחרים: אֱדוֹם) וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי מַהוּ לְשׁוֹן אֲמֻצִּים, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם ״קִטְמָנִין״:


**ash- colored** to cause Edom and Ishmael to mount, but I do not know the expression אֲמֻצִּים. Jonathan rendered: ash-colored.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:5:1)


**אֵלֶּה אַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הַשָּׁמַיִם.** שָׂרֵי הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת מַלְכֻיּוֹת הַקַּדְמוֹנִיּוֹת הַמּוֹשְׁלוֹת בְּאַרְבַּע רוּחוֹת הַשָּׁמַיִם:


**These are the four corners of the heavens** The heavenly princes of the nations that rule over the four corners of the heavens.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:5:2)


**יוֹצְאוֹת מֵהִתְיַצֵּב וְגוֹ׳.** בָּאוּ לְפָנָיו וְנָתַן לָהֶם רְשׁוּת מִפִּיו לִמְשֹׁל:


**coming forth from standing, etc.** They came before Him, and He gave them permission from His mouth to rule.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:6:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בָּהּ הַסּוּסִים הַשְּׁחֹרִים יוֹצְאִים אֶל אֶרֶץ צָפוֹן.** לְהַרְכִּיב אֶת מָדַי. וְעַל הָאֲדוּמִים לֹא כָּתַב יוֹצְאִים, שֶׁכְּבָר כָּלְתָה לָהּ מֶמְשֶׁלֶת בָּבֶל:


**The one [chariot] the black horses were in was going to the northland** to cause Media to ride. Concerning the red ones, he does not write that they were going forth because the kingdom of Babylon had already been destroyed.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:6:2)


**וְהַלְּבָנִים יָצְאוּ אֶל אַחֲרֵיהֶם.** לְהַרְכִּיב אֶת פָּרַס, שֶׁשְּׁתֵּיהֶן בַּצָּפוֹן, וּמָשְׁלוּ עַל בָּבֶל:


**and the white ones went forth after them** to cause Persia to mount both of them, in the north, and they ruled over Babylon.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:6:3)


**וְהַבְּרֻדִּים יָצְאוּ אֶל אֶרֶץ הַתֵּימָן.** לְהַרְכִּיב אֶת יָוָן וְיִטְּלוּ הַמְּלוּכָה מִיַּד פָּרַס. וּבְפִתְרוֹנוֹ לְרַב סְעַדְיָה בְּדָנִיֵּאל רָאִיתִי מֶלֶךְ הַנֶּגֶב הוּא מֶלֶךְ יָוָן (דניאל יא:ה):


**and the spotted ones went forth to the southland** to cause Greece to mount so that Greece should take the kingdom from Persia. In Rav Saadiah’s interpretation of Daniel, the king of the south (11:5) is identified with the king of Greece.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:7:1)


**וְהָאֲמֻצִּים יָצְאוּ וַיְבַקְשׁוּ.** מִלְּפָנָיו שֶׁיְּהֵא אֹרֶךְ גָּדוֹל לְמֶמְשַׁלְתָּם, לָלֶכֶת בְּכָל הָאָרֶץ:


**And the ash-colored ones went forth and begged** of Him that they have a very long rule, to go throughout all the earth.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:7:2)


**וַיֹּאמֶר לְכוּ הִתְהַלְּכוּ בָאָרֶץ.** נָתַן לָהֶם רְשׁוּת לִמְשֹׁל מִמְשָׁל רַב (דניאל יא:ג), וְהוּא מַלְכוּת אֱדוֹם:


**And He said, “Go, walk to and fro on the earth”** He gave them permission to rule a great ruling, and that is the kingdom of Edom.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:8:1)


**רְאֵה הַיּוֹצְאִים אֶל אֶרֶץ צָפוֹן.** לְהַרְכִּיב מָדַי וּפָרָס:


**Look, those going forth to the northland** to cause Media and Persia to mount.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:8:2)


**הֵנִיחוּ אֶת רוּחִי.** שִׁכְּכוּ אֶת חֲמָתִי בְּבָבֶל, שֶׁהִשְׁחִיתוּ אֶת בֵּלְשַׁצַּר וְזַרְעוֹ שֶׁל נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר (דניאל ה:ל). הוּא שֶׁאָמַר (ישעיהו מח:יד) ״ה׳ אֲהֵבוֹ יַעֲשֶׂה חֶפְצוֹ בְּבָבֶל וְגוֹ׳״:


**eased My spirit** They caused My anger to subside in Babylon when they destroyed Belshazzar and Nebuchadnezzar’s descendants. This is what Isaiah (48:14) said: “The Lord loves him who shall do His will in Babylon, etc.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:10:1)


**לָקוֹחַ מֵאֵת הַגּוֹלָה מֵחֶלְדַּי וְגוֹ׳,** אֲשֶׁר בָּאוּ מִבָּבֶל. כָּל הָאֲנָשִׁים הָאֵלֶּה:


**Take from the exiles - from Heldai, etc., who have come from Babylon** All these people.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:12:1)


**צֶמַח שְׁמוֹ.** הוּא זְרֻבָּבֶל הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה ״הִנְנִי מֵבִיא אֶת עַבְדִּי צֶמַח״ (זכריה ג:ח), וְעַל שֵׁם שֶׁצָּמְחָה גְּדֻלָּתוֹ מְעַט מְעַט. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִים אוֹתוֹ (תנחומא קרח יב) בְּמֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ, אֲבָל כָּל הָעִנְיָן הַזֶּה מְדַבֵּר בְּבַיִת שֵׁנִי:


**whose name is the Shoot** He is Zerubbabel, mentioned above (3:8): “Behold, I bring My servant, the Shoot,” since his greatness burgeoned little by little. Some interpret this as referring to the King Messiah, but the entire context deals with the [time of the] Second Temple.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:12:2)


**וּמִתַּחְתָּיו יִצְמָח.** מִזֶּרַע הַמְּלוּכָה:


**who will spring up out of his place** From royal descent.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:13:1)


**וְהוּא יִשָּׂא הוֹד.** הוֹד נְשִׂיאוּת:


**and he shall bear glory** The glory of the kingship.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:13:2)


**וַיֵּשֶׁב.** כֹּהֵן גָּדוֹל עַל כִּסֵּא הַכְּהֻנָּה:


**And he shall sit** The High Priest [shall sit] on the throne of the priesthood.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:13:3)


**וַעֲצַת שָׁלוֹם וְגוֹ׳.** הַנָּשִׂיא וְהַכֹּהֵן יֹאהֲבוּ זֶה אֶת זֶה:


**And a council of peace, etc.** The king and the Priest shall love one another.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:14:1)


**וְהָעֲטָרוֹת תִּהְיֶה לְחֵלֶם וְגוֹ׳.** מִקְרָא מְסֹרָס הוּא, וְהָעֲטָרוֹת תִּהְיֶינָה בְּהֵיכַל ה׳ לְזִכָּרוֹן טוֹב לְחֵלֶם וּלְטוֹבִיָּה אֲשֶׁר הִתְנַדְּבוּ הַכֶּסֶף וְהַזָּהָב. וְאוֹתָן עֲטָרוֹת הָיוּ תְּלוּיוֹת בַּחֲלוֹנוֹת בְּגֹבַהּ הַהֵיכָל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מִדּוֹת (מידות ג,ח):


**And the crowns shall be for Helem, etc.** This is a transposed verse. It should be understood as: And the crowns shall be in the Temple of the Lord as a good memorial for Helem and for Tobijah, who donated the silver and the gold. Those crowns were hanging in the windows in the height of the Temple, as we learned in tractate Middoth (36a, 3:8).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:14:2)


**חֵלֶם.** הוּא חֶלְדָּי (זכריה ו:י):


**for Helem** That is Heldai.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Zechariah 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/6:15:1)


**וְהָיָה אִם שָׁמוֹעַ.** וְהָיָה לָכֶם הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה **אִם שָׁמוֹעַ תִּשְׁמְעוּ וְגוֹ׳**:


**And it shall come to pass if you hearken** And this thing shall come to pass for you if you hearken, etc.



## Chapter 7


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:2:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח בֵּית אֵל שַׂרְאֶצֶר וְרֶגֶם מֶלֶךְ וַאֲנָשָׁיו.** אֲנָשִׁים צַדִּיקִים הָיוּ, וְשָׁלְחוּ מִבָּבֶל לִקְרוֹבֵיהֶם שֶׁבְּבֵית אֵל לָבֹא לְחַלּוֹת אֶת פְּנֵי ה׳ בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם בַּעֲדָם, וְלִשְׁאֹל מֵאֵת הַכֹּהֲנִים לְהוֹדִיעָם אִם יִבְכּוּ בְּחֹדֶשׁ אָב, אַחֲרֵי אֲשֶׁר חָזַר הַבַּיִת לְהִבָּנוֹת:


**And Sharezer and Regem Melech and his men sent to Bethel** They were righteous men, and they sent [word] to their kinsmen in Bethel to come to pray before the Lord in Jerusalem for them, and to ask the priests to let them know if they should [still] weep in the month of Av, since the Temple had been rebuilt.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:3:1)


**הַנָּזֵר.** לִפְרוֹשׁ מִן הַתַּעֲנוּג כַּאֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתִי זֶה שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה. אֵין נְזִירָה בְּכָל מָקוֹם אֶלָּא פְּרִישׁוּת (ספרא אמור פרשה ד, א):


**abstaining** To abstain from pleasure, as I have done these seventy years. The word נְזִירָה always means separation.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:5:1)


**וּבַשְּׁבִיעִי.** הוּא צוֹם גְּדַלְיָה (ראש השנה יח:):


**and in the seventh [month]** That is the fast of Gedaliah.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:5:2)


**הֲצוֹם צַמְתֻּנִי אָנִי.** הֵ״א נְקוּדָה חֲטַף פַּתָּח, לְפִי שֶׁהִיא מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת לְשׁוֹן תְּמִיהָא: שֶׁמָּא לִכְבוֹדִי צַמְתֶּם, שֶׁתִּהְיוּ צְרִיכִים לָצוּם גַּם עַתָּה?:


**did you fast for Me, even for Me** The ‘he’ is vowelized with a ‘hataf pattah’ because it is used as the interrogative: Did you fast in My honor that you should be required to fast now, too?



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:6:1)


**וְכִי תֹאכְלוּ וְכִי תִשְׁתּוּ.** וְאִם לֹא תָצוּמוּ, מַה לִּי גַּם בָּזוֹ? הֲלֹא אַתֶּם לַהֲנָאַתְכֶם הוּא הַמַּאֲכָל וְהַמִּשְׁתֶּה:


**And when you eat and when you drink** And, if you do not fast, that, too, means nothing to Me. Are not the food and the drink for your own pleasure?



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:7:1)


**הֲלֹא אֶת הַדְּבָרִים.** מִי גָּרַם לָכֶם הַצּוֹם וְהַמִּסְפֵּד? הֲלֹא דִבְרֵי תוֹכָחוֹת **אֲשֶׁר קָרָא ה׳ וְגוֹ׳**, הֵם הֶחֱרִיבוּ אֶתְכֶם, וְעַל כֵּן צַמְתֶּם. וְאֵלֶּה הַדְּבָרִים אֲשֶׁר קָרָא: ״מִשְׁפַּט אֱמֶת שְׁפֹטוּ״ (זכריה ז:ט):


**Was it not the words** Who caused you the fast and the mourning? Was it not the words of reproof that the Lord proclaimed, etc.? They brought about your destruction, and therefore you fasted. And these are the words that He proclaimed: Execute true judgment.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:12:1)


**שָׂמוּ שָׁמִיר.** תּוֹלַעַת חָזָק מִצּוּר, מַרְאִין אוֹתוֹ עַל הָאֶבֶן וְהִיא מִתְבַּקַּעַת (סוטה מח:):


**shamir** A worm stronger than flint. They would show it to the stone, which would split.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:13:1)


**וַיְהִי כַּאֲשֶׁר קָרָא וְלֹא שָׁמֵעוּ.** אָמַר ה׳ צְבָאוֹת עֲלֵיהֶן בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים **כֵּן יִקְרְאוּ וְלֹא אֶשְׁמָע**:


**And it came about; as He called and they did not hearken** the Lord said concerning them in those days: So shall they call and I will not hearken.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:14:1)


**וָאֵסָעֲרֵם.** בַּגָּלוּת, וְכֵן עָשִׂיתִי:


**I will scatter them** in exile. And so I did.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:14:2)


**וַיָּשִׂימוּ.** בַּעֲוֹנָם:


**they made** with their iniquity.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 7:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/7:14:3)


**אֶרֶץ חֶמְדָּה לְשַׁמָּה.** וְהוּא גָּרַם לָהֶם הַצּוֹם וְהַמִּסְפֵּד, אֲבָל עַתָּה: ״קִנֵּאתִי לְצִיּוֹן״ (זכריה ח:ב) – בִּשְׁבִיל צִיּוֹן אֲקַנֵּא אֶת הַכַּשְׂדִּים:


**desolation** And that brought about the fast (and the mourning), but now [I am jealous for Zion, etc.]



## Chapter 8


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:2:1)


**קִנֵּאתִי לָהּ.** בִּשְׁבִילָהּ, וְלֹא תִזָּקְקוּ לָצוּם וְלִסְפֹּד. וְכֵן סוֹף הָעִנְיָן ״צוֹם הַחֲמִישִׁי וְגוֹמֵר״ (זכריה ח:יט):


**I am jealous for her** For [not ‘to’] her. And you will not need to fast and to mourn. And so is the end of the chapter: “The fast of the fifth month, etc.”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:4:1)


**מֵרֹב יָמִים.** יִהְיוּ צְרִיכִים לְמִשְׁעֶנֶת בְּיָדָם:


**because of old age** they will require the staff in their hand.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:6:1)


**כִּי יִפָּלֵא.** בְּעֵינֵיהֶם הַחֶסֶד הַגָּדוֹל אֲשֶׁר אֶעֱשֶׂה לָהֶם:


**As it will be wonderful** in their eyes, the great loving-kindness that I will do for them.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:6:2)


**גַּם בְּעֵינַי יִפָּלֵא.** אֵיךְ שַׁבְתִּי מִן הֶחָרוֹן הַגָּדוֹל. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ עַל שְׁחִיטַת יֵצֶר הָרָע בְּמַסֶּכֶת סֻכָּה (סוכה נב.): **בְּעֵינַי יִפָּלֵא** אֵיךְ יוּכְלוּ הַצַּדִּיקִים לַעֲמֹד כְּנֶגְדּוֹ בְּחַיֵּיהֶם:


**it will also be wonderful in My eyes** how I returned from the great wrath. And our Sages expounded on [this phrase], concerning the slaughter of the evil inclination, in tractate Sukkah (52a): **it will also be wonderful in My eyes** how the righteous will be able to stand up against [the evil inclination] during their lifetime.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:9:1)


**תֶּחֱזַקְנָה יְדֵיכֶם.** לִבְנוֹת וּלְהַשְׁלִים, וְאַל תִּירְאוּ מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ הַמְרַפִּים יְדֵיכֶם וּמְבַהִילִים אֶתְכֶם מִלִּבְנוֹת, וּכְמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא (עזרא ד:ד):


**Let your hands be strong** to build and to complete [the Temple]; and do not fear the people of the land, who are weakening your hands and frightening you from building it, as is related in the Book of Ezra (4:4).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:9:2)


**הַשּׁוֹמְעִים בַּיָּמִים הָאֵלֶּה.** בִּשְׁנַת אַרְבַּע לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ:


**you who hear in these days** In the fourth year of Darius


###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:9:3)


**אֶת הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה.** שֶׁל נִחוּמִים מִפִּי הַנְּבִיאִים אֲשֶׁר נִבְּאוּ לָהֶם אָז **בְּיוֹם יֻסַּד בֵּית ה׳** בִּשְׁנַת שְׁתַּיִם לְדָרְיָוֶשׁ (חגי א:טו) אֶת הַדְּבָרִים לְהַתְחִיל בַּמְּלָאכָה. כִּי נִבְחֲנוּ אוֹתָם הַנְּבִיאִים בְּעֵינֵיהֶם בְּדִבְרֵיהֶם, שֶׁהֵם נְבִיאֵי אֱמֶת, וּלְפִיכָךְ כְּדַאי הֵם לִסְמֹךְ עֲלֵיהֶם אַף בְּדִבְרֵי נִחוּמִים שֶׁל עַכְשָׁיו. וּבַמֶּה נִבְחֲנוּהָ? **כִּי לִפְנֵי הַיָּמִים הָהֵם שְׂכַר הָאָדָם וְגוֹמֵר.** וְהֵם נִבְּאוּ לָכֶם שֶׁמִּשֶּׁתַּתְחִילוּ בַּמְּלָאכָה תָּבֹא בְּרָכָה בְּמַעֲשֵׂה יְדֵיכֶם, וְנִתְקַיְּמוּ דִּבְרֵיהֶם, שֶׁהֲרֵי **עַתָּה לֹא כַיָּמִים הָרִאשֹׁנִים**:


**these words** of consolations from the mouths of the prophets who prophesied for [the people] - on the day the foundation of the house of the Lord was laid, in the second year of Darius - to begin the work. Those prophets were proven with their words to be true prophets in your eyes; therefore, it is also worthy to rely on them for the words of consolation concerning the present time. And in what way were they proven? **For before those days, there was no hire for man, etc.** They prophesied for you that when you would begin the work, a blessing would come into the work of your hands; and their words were fulfilled, for now it is not like the early days.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:14:1)


**וְלֹא נִחָמְתִּי.** עַל אֲשֶׁר זַמֹּתִי לְהָבִיא עֲלֵיכֶם לַחֲזוֹר בִּי, אֶלָּא הֲבִיאוֹתִיהָ. כֵּן לֹא אֶתְנַחֵם עַל הַטּוֹבָה אֲשֶׁר זָמַמְתִּי לָכֶם:


**and I did not repent** of what I had planned to bring upon you, [or] retract it; but I brought it. Neither will I repent of the good that I have planned for you.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:16:1)


**וּמִשְׁפַּט שָׁלוֹם.** הִיא הַפְּשָׁרָה:


**and judgment of peace** I.e., compromise.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:17:1)


**וְאִישׁ אֶת רָעַת רֵעֵהוּ.** הִזְהִירָם עַל שִׂנְאַת חִנָּם, וְהִיא שֶׁהֶחֱרִיבָה בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ שֵׁנִי (יומא ט:):


**And let no one think evil of his neighbors** He warned them concerning hatred without cause, which brought about the destruction of the Second Temple.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:1)


**צוֹם הָרְבִיעִי.** צוֹם שֶׁל תַּמּוּז, שֶׁהוּא רְבִיעִי לֶחֳדָשִׁים:


**the fast of the fourth [month]** The fast of Tammuz, which is the fourth of the months.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:2)


**צוֹם הַחֲמִישִׁי.** שֶׁל אָב:


**the fast of the fifth [month]** of Av.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:3)


**וְצוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי.** שְׁלֹשָׁה בְּתִשְׁרֵי, שֶׁבּוֹ נֶהֱרַג גְּדַלְיָה (מלכים ב כה:כה; ירמיהו מא:א-ב):


**the fast of the seventh [month]** The third of Tishri, when Gedaliah was assassinated.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:19:4)


**וְצוֹם הָעֲשִׂירִי.** שֶׁל טֵבֵת (ראש השנה יח:):


**and the fast of the tenth [month]** Of Teveth.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Zechariah 8:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/8:23:1)


**עֲשָׂרָה אֲנָשִׁים.** מִשִּׁבְעִים לָשׁוֹן, הֲרֵי שְׁבַע מֵאוֹת לְכָל כָּנָף וְכָנָף. הֲרֵי לְאַרְבַּע כַּנְפֵי הַטַּלִּית אַלְפַּיִם וּשְׁמוֹנֶה מֵאוֹת (שבת לב:):


**ten men** from the seventy nations. This equals seven hundred for each corner. For the four corners of the tallith there will be two thousand and eight hundred.



## Chapter 9


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:1:1)


**בְּאֶרֶץ חַדְרָךְ.** דָּרַשׁ רַבִּי יְהוּדָה בַּר אֶלְעָאִי: זֶה מָשִׁיחַ, שֶׁהוּא חַד לְעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת וְרַךְ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל. אָמַר לוֹ רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בֶּן דּוֹרְמַסְקִית: יְהוּדָה בְּרִבִּי, עַד מָתַי אַתָּה מְעַוֵּת עָלֵינוּ אֶת הַכְּתוּבִים? מֵעִיד אֲנִי עָלַי שָׁמַיִם וָאָרֶץ, שֶׁאֲנִי מִדַּמֶּשֶׂק, וְיֵשׁ מָקוֹם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ חַדְרָךְ. וּמָה אֲנִי מְקַיֵּם **וְדַמֶּשֶׂק מְנֻחָתוֹ**? שֶׁעֲתִידָה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם לִהְיוֹת מַגַּעַת עַד דַּמֶּשֶׂק, וְאֵין מְנוּחָתוֹ אֶלָּא יְרוּשָׁלַיִם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״זֹאת מְנוּחָתִי עֲדֵי עַד״ (תהלים קלב:יד). אָמַר לוֹ: וּמָה אֲנִי מְקַיֵּם ״וְנִבְנְתָה עִיר עַל תִּלָּהּ״ (ירמיהו ל:יח)? אָמַר לוֹ: שֶׁאֵין עֲתִידָה לָזוּז מִמְּקוֹמָהּ. כָּךְ בְּרַיְתָא שְׁנוּיָה בְּסִפְרֵי (ספרי דברים א:א):


**In the land of Hadrach** R’ Judah the son of Ilai expounded: This is the Messiah, who is sharp against the nations and soft to Israel. R’ Yose the son of a Damascene mother said to him [to Judah the son of Ilai]: “Judah, our master, how long will you pervert the verses for us? I call heaven and earth to testify for me, that I am from Damascus, and there is a place named Hadrach. [Rabbi Yose continued:] Now how do I explain: ‘And Damascus is His resting place?’” That Jerusalem is destined to reach as far as Damascus. ‘His resting place’ means only Jerusalem, as it is said: (Psalms 132:14) “This is My resting place to eternity.” He said to him [i.e. R’ Judah to R’ Yose]: “Now how do I explain (Jer. 30: 18) ‘And the city shall be built on its mound?’” He replied: “That it is not destined to move from its place.” So was the baraitha taught in Sifre (Deut. 1:1).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:1:2)


**כִּי לַה׳ עֵין אָדָם.** כִּי בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא יִשְׁעֶה הָאָדָם עַל עוֹשֵׂהוּ וְעֵינָיו אֶל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא תִּרְאֶינָה (ישעיהו יז:ז), כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לְמַעְלָה ״נֵלְכָה עִמָּכֶם״ (זכריה ח:כג), וְנִסְפְּחוּ הֵם וְעָרֵיהֶם עַל עָרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**for man’s eye shall be to the Lord** For on that day man will turn to his Maker, and his eyes will see the Holy One, blessed be He, as [the prophet] states above: Let us go with you. And they and their cities will join the cities of Israel.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:1:3)


**וְכָל שִׁבְטֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.** חֲשׁוּבִים לַה׳ עַל כָּל הָאֻמּוֹת הַקַּדְמוֹנִים. כְּיוֹצֵא בוֹ: ״וְשָׁלֹמֹה אָהַב נָשִׁים נָכְרִיּוֹת רַבּוֹת וְאֶת בַּת פַּרְעֹה״ (מלכים א יא:א), וַהֲלֹא בַּת פַּרְעֹה בִּכְלָל הָיְתָה? אֶלָּא שֶׁהָיְתָה בַּת פַּרְעֹה חֲבִיבָה לוֹ כְּנֶגֶד כֻּלָּם. אַף כָּאן, כָּל הָאֻמּוֹת יִהְיוּ לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, וְיִשְׂרָאֵל כְּנֶגֶד כּוּלָם:


**and all the tribes of Israel** They are regarded by God above all the nations. Similarly, Scripture states (I Kings 11: 1): “King Solomon loved many foreign women and the daughter of Pharaoh.” Now, was the daughter of Pharaoh not included in the generalization?
