But the daughter of Pharaoh was as dear to him as all of them. Here, too, all the nations shall belong to the Holy One, blessed be He, and Israel is equal to all of them.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:2:1)


**וְגַם חֲמָת תִּגְבָּל בָּהּ.** תַּשִּׂיג גְּבוּלָהּ בִּגְבוּל יְרוּשָׁלָיִם:


**And also Hamath shall border thereon** Its border shall reach the border of Jerusalem


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:2:2)


**צוֹר וְצִידוֹן.** גַּם הֵמָּה בְּנוֹתֶיהָ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁ**חָכְמָה מְאֹד** שֶׁנִּתְחַכְּמָה מְאֹד, וּסְבוּרָה לִהְיוֹת לְעוֹלָם גְּבֶרֶת בְּחָכְמָתָהּ:


**Tyre and Sidon** They, too, shall be its [Jerusalem’s] villages, although it has become exceedingly wise. She [Tyre] became exceedingly wise and thought to be forever a mistress with her wisdom.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:3:1)


**וַתִּבֶן צוֹר מָצוֹר לָהּ.** וְאוֹמֶרֶת: ״מִי יוֹרִידֵנִי לָאָרֶץ״? (עובדיה א:ג):


**And Tyre built a fortification for herself** and [Tyre] says, “Who will bring me down to earth?”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:3:2)


**וַתִּצְבֹּר כֶּסֶף כֶּעָפָר.** וּמִתְהַלֶּלֶת בְּעָשְׁרָהּ׃


**and she gathered silver like dust** and boasts of her wealth.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:3:3)


**וְחָרוּץ.** מִין מַרְאִית זָהָב׃


**and gold** a certain appearance of gold.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:4:1)


**הִנֵּה אֲדֹ׳.** שֶׁהוּא שַׁלִּיט עַל כֹּל׃


**Behold, the Lord** Who is the ruler over all.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:4:2)


**יוֹרִישֶׁנָּה.** לְשׁוֹן ״מוֹרִישׁ וּמַעֲשִׁיר״ (שמואל א ב:ז):


**shall impoverish her** an expression similar to (I Sam. 2: 7) “impoverishes and makes rich.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:4:3)


**וְהִכָּה בַיָּם חֵילָהּ.** וְהִבְלִיעַ בְּתוֹךְ יַמִּים מָמוֹנָהּ, יַעֲלֶה עָלֶיהָ אֶת הַיָּם וְיַטְבִּיעֶנָּה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר יְחֶזְקֵאל (יחזקאל כז:כו-כז):


**He shall smite her wealth in the sea** And shall cause her wealth to be swallowed in the midst of the seas. He shall cause the sea to rise over it and sink it, as Ezekiel said (27).



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:5:1)


**תִּרְאֶה אַשְׁקְלוֹן.** אֶת מַפֶּלֶת צוֹר **וְתִירָא**:


**Ashkelon shall see** the downfall of Tyre and fear.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:5:2)


**וְעֶקְרוֹן** אַף הִיא **תִּירָא:**


**and Ekron** too, shall fear.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:5:3)


**כִּי הוֹבִישׁ מֶבָּטָהּ.** שֶׁהָיְתָה נִשְׁעֶנֶת עַל צוֹר, שֶׁהָיְתָה רֹאשׁ לִבְנֵי עֵשָׂו. וְעֶקְרוֹן אַף הִיא לֶאֱדוֹם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת מְגִלָּה (מגילה ו.). וּתְחִלָּה הָיְתָה מִשֶּׁל פְּלִשְׁתִּים:


**for the one to whom she looked was ashamed** For [Ashkelon] relied on Tyre, which was the head of the sons of Esau, and Ekron, too, belonged to Edom, as our Sages said in tractate Megillah (6a): “Ekron shall be uprooted (Zeph. 2:4).” This is Caesarea, which belongs to Edom, and it originally belonged to the Philistines.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:6:1)


**וְיָשַׁב מַמְזֵר בְּאַשְׁדּוֹד.** וְיָשַׁב עַם נָכְרִי **בְּאַשְׁדּוֹד**, הֵם יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהָיוּ בָּהּ נָכְרִים (קידושין עב:):


**And the strangers shall dwell in Ashdod** And a strange people shall dwell in Ashdod. Those are the Israelites, who were strange in it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:1)


**וַהֲסִרֹתִי דָמָיו מִפִּיו.** שֶׁל עֵשָׂו אֵלּוּ בֵּית בִּימָה שֶׁלָּהֶם שֶׁהָיוּ זוֹרְקִים שָׁם דְּמֵי זִבְחֵיהֶם (מגילה ו.). וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין שְׁפִיכוּת דָּמִים שֶׁהָיָה שׁוֹפֵךְ דְּמֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**And I will remove his blood from his mouth** From the mouth of Esau. This is their house of Bamia [one of Edom’s principal deities (Rashi to Megillah 6a)], where they would sprinkle the blood of their sacrifices. Others interpret this to mean the bloodshed, that the [Edomites] would shed the blood of Israel.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:2)


**וְנִשְׁאַר גַּם הוּא לֵאלֹהֵינוּ.** אֵלּוּ בָּתֵּי כְּנֵסִיּוֹת וּבָתֵּי מִדְרָשׁוֹת שֶׁבַּגָּלוּת (מגילה כט.):


**and it, too, shall remain to our God** These are the synagogues and the studyhalls in our exile.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:3)


**וְהָיָה כְּאַלּוּף בִּיהוּדָה.** אֵלּוּ טַרְטִיָּאוֹת וְקַרְקְסִיָּאוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶם, שֶׁעֲתִידִים שָׂרֵי יְהוּדָה לְלַמֵּד בָּהֶם תּוֹרָה בָּרַבִּים:


**and it shall be like a study hall in Judah** These are their theaters and circuses, where the princes of Judah are destined to teach Torah in public.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:4)


**וְהָיָה כְּאַלּוּף.** לְשׁוֹן אִלּוּף וְלִמּוּד:


**and it shall be like a study hall** an expression of study and learning.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:5)


**וְעֶקְרוֹן.** תִּהְיֶה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל:


**and Ekron** shall be to Israel.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:7:6)


**כִּיבוּסִי.** יְרוּשָׁלַיִם:


**like Jebusi** Jerusalem.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:8:1)


**וְחָנִיתִי לְבֵיתִי מִצָּבָה.** אֶחֱנֶה אֵצֶל בֵּיתִי לְהָגֵן עָלֶיהָ מִן הַמַּעֲמִידִים מִבְצָר וּמַשְׁחִית, שֶׁלֹּא יַצִּיבוּ עָלֶיהָ. וְדֻגְמָתוֹ ״וְכָל צֹבֶיהָ וּמְצֹדָתָהּ״ (ישעיהו כט:ז). וְיֵשׁ עוֹד לִפְתֹּר מִצָּבָה כְּמוֹ מִצָּבָא בְּאָלֶ״ף:


**And I will encamp beside My house against a garrison** I will encamp beside My house to protect it from those who set up a garrison and raiders, that they should not set these up against it. A similar word is used in (Isa. 29:7) “and all those stationed around her, and those who trap her.” It is also possible to interpret מִצָבָה as מִצָבָא, with an ‘alef’ [meaning against a host].


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:8:2)


**כִּי עַתָּה רָאִיתִי.** צָרָתָם בְּעֵינַי, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעַד הֵנָּה הִסְתַּרְתִּי פָנַי מֵהֶם:


**for now I have seen with My eyes** Now I have seen their distress with My eyes, although until now I have hidden My countenance from them.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:1)


**הִנֵּה מַלְכֵּךְ יָבוֹא לָךְ.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר לְפוֹתְרוֹ אֶלָּא עַל מֶלֶךְ הַמָּשִׁיחַ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בּוֹ **וּמָשְׁלוֹ מִיָּם עַד יָם**, וְלֹא מָצִינוּ מוֹשֵׁל לְיִשְׂרָאֵל כָּזֶה בִּימֵי בַּיִת שֵׁנִי:


**Behold! Your king shall come to you** It is impossible to interpret this except as referring to the King Messiah, as it is stated: “and his rule shall be from sea to sea.” We do not find that Israel had such a ruler during the days of the Second Temple.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:2)


**צַדִּיק וְנוֹשָׁע.** בַּה׳׃


**just and victorious** saved by the Lord.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:3)


**עָנִי.** עֲנְוְתָן:


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:4)


**וְרוֹכֵב עַל חֲמוֹר.** מִדַּת עֲנָוָה הִיא:


**and riding a donkey** This is a symbol of humility.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:9:5)


**וְעַל עַיִר.** כְּמוֹ ״וַעֲיָרִים עֲשָׂרָה״ (בראשית לב:טז):


**and a foal of she-donkeys** as in (Gen. 32:16) “and ten foals.”



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:1)


**וְהִכְרַתִּי רֶכֶב.** שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ צְרִיכִין לָהּ:


**And I will cut off the chariots** for they will not need them.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:2)


**וּמָשְׁלוֹ.** וּמִמְשָׁל שֶׁלּוֹ:


**and his rule** וּמָשְׁלוֹ


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:3)


**מִיָּם וְעַד יָם.** ״מִיַּמָּא וְעַד מַעֲרְבָא״, לְסוֹף הָעוֹלָם (בראשית רבה ע:יט):


**from the sea to the west** From its sea to the west, to the end of the world.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:10:4)


**וּמִנָּהָר.** פְּרָת:


**and from the river** The Euphrates.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:11:1)


**גַּם אַתְּ.** תִּוָּשְׁעִי עִמּוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא צַדִּיק וְנוֹשָׁע (זכריה ט:ט), כִּי **בְדַם בְּרִיתֵךְ** הֲרֵי נִבֵּאתִי לָכֶם לֶעָתִיד, וְגַם עַתָּה מִגָּלוּת בָּבֶל שֶׁאַתֶּם עַכְשָׁיו בְּתוֹכָהּ:


**You, too** shall be saved with him, as he is just and victorious. For with the blood of your covenant, I prophesied for you for the future; and for now, too, from the Babylonian exile in which you are now found.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:11:2)


**בְּדַם בְּרִיתֵךְ.** בִּזְכוּת דַּם הַבְּרִית שֶׁנִּזְרַק עֲלֵיכֶם בְּסִינַי, **הִנֵּה דַם הַבְּרִית אֲשֶׁר כָּרַת** (שמות כד:ח):


**with the blood of your covenant** With the merit of the blood of the covenant that was sprinkled upon you at Sinai, as it is written (Exod. 24:8): “Behold the blood of the covenant that the Lord has made.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:11:3)


**שִׁלַּחְתִּי אֲסִירַיִךְ.** מִן הַגָּלוּת (ויקרא רבה יט:ו)׃


**I have freed your prisoners** from the exile.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:12:1)


**שׁוּבוּ לְבִצָּרוֹן.** לְכֹחֲכֶם וְלִכְבוֹדְכֶם, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאַתֶּם עַתָּה בִּרְשׁוּת מַלְכֵי פָּרַס, אַתֶּם **אֲסִירֵי הַתִּקְוָה.** אֲשֶׁר קִוִּיתֶם לִי עַד הֵנָּה לְמַלֹּאות דְּבָרַי לְקֵץ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה (ירמיה כט:י):


**Return to the stronghold** To your strength and to your glory, even though you are now under the rule of the kings of Persia. **you prisoners of hope** who hoped for Me until now that My words [would] be fulfilled at the end of seventy years.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:12:2)


**גַּם הַיּוֹם מַגִּיד מִשְׁנֶה אָשִׁיב לָךְ.** עוֹד **הַיּוֹם** אֲנִי **מַגִּיד** לְךָ בְּשׂוֹרָה שְׁנִיָּה, לְבַד זוֹ שֶׁל בִּנְיָן. וּמַה הִיא הַגָּדָה הַשְּׁנִיָּה שֶׁאֲנִי שָׁב לִשְׁנוֹת לָכֶם?:


**Also today, I will restore to you a double promise** Today I will yet tell you second tidings, besides those of the building. And what is the second narrative that I am returning to repeat to you?



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:13:1)


**כִּי דָרַכְתִּי לִי יְהוּדָה.** סוֹף שֶׁאַנְטִיוֹכוּס יִטֹּל הַמְּלוּכָה מִיַּד מַלְכֵי פָרַס וְיָרֵעוּ לָכֶם, וַאֲנִי אֶדְרֹךְ יְהוּדָה לִהְיוֹת לִי כְּקֶשֶׁת מִלְחָמָה, וְיִלָּחֲמוּ בְּאַנְטִיוֹכוּס בִּימֵי חַשְׁמוֹנָאִים:


**For I bend Judah for Me** Eventually, the Greeks will wrest the kingdom from the Persian kings and inflict harm upon you, and I will bend Judah to be a war bow for me; and Judah will wage war against the Greeks in the days of the Hasmoneans.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:13:2)


**קֶשֶׁת מִלֵּאתִי אֶפְרָיִם.** מִקְרָא קָצָר הוּא, כְּמוֹ קֶשֶׁת מִלֵּאתִי יַד אֶפְרָיִם, כְּמוֹ ״וְיֵהוּא מִלֵּא יָדוֹ בַקֶּשֶׁת״ (מלכים ב ט:כד). אֶפְרָיִם יִהְיֶה לִי כְּאַשְׁפָּה מְלֵאָה חִצִּים:


**a bow; I filled [the hand of] Ephraim** This is an elliptical verse: Like a bow, I filled the hand of Ephraim; as (II Kings 9:24), “And Jehu put all his strength into his bow.” Ephraim shall be as a quiver full of arrows to Me.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:13:3)


**וְעוֹרַרְתִּי בָנַיִךְ צִיּוֹן.** עַל חֵיל אַנְטִיוֹכוּס (פסיקתא רבתי ב):


**and I will arouse your children, O Zion** The children of Zion on the children of Javan.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:14:1)


**וַה׳ עֲלֵיהֶם יֵרָאֶה.** בְּסֵפֶר יוֹסִיפוֹן מָצִינוּ: בָּעֵת הַהִיא נִרְאָה מוֹפֵת לְאַנְשֵׁי יְרוּשָׁלָיִם:


**And the Lord shall appear over them** In the book of Josippon (chap. 18), we find that at that time a sign appeared to the people of Jerusalem.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:14:2)


**בַּשּׁוֹפָר יִתְקָע.** יִרְעַם:


**shall sound the shofar** He shall thunder.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:14:3)


**בְּסַעֲרוֹת תֵּימָן.** לְסַעֵר אֶת אַנְשֵׁי תֵימָן, הֵם חֵיל אַנְטִיוֹכוּס. וְיֵשׁ מֵרַבּוֹתֵינוּ (ירושלמי תענית ב,ד) שֶׁפָּתְרוּהוּ עַל אֱדוֹם, לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא:


**and He shall go with the whirlwinds of the south** To storm the people of the south; they are the Greeks. And some of our Sages interpreted [the phrase] as referring to Edom in the future.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:1)


**וְאָכְלוּ.** שְׁלַל אוֹיְבֵיהֶם:


**and they shall devour** the spoils of their enemies.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:2)


**וְכָבְשׁוּ.** תַּחְתֶּיהָ **אַבְנֵי קָלַע**, הֵם הַיְּוָנִים שֶׁהֵם מְלֻמְּדֵי קֶשֶׁת וְלִקְלֹעַ בָּאֶבֶן:


**and they shall tread** under them **sling-stones** They are the Greeks, who are trained to use the bow and to sling stones.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:3)


**וְהָמוּ כְּמוֹ יָיִן.** הֲמִיּוֹן בְּקוֹל שָׂשׂוֹן וְשִׂמְחָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹמִים שְׁתוּיֵי יָיִן:


**They shall make a noise like [those who drink] wine** They shall make noise with a voice of joy and happiness, as those who have drunk much wine make noise.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:4)


**וּמָלְאוּ.** נַפְשׁוֹתָם כָּל טוֹב:


**and they shall become full** Their souls shall become full of all good.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:5)


**כַּמִּזְרָק.** שֶׁמָּלֵא דָם לִפְנֵי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**like the basin** that is full of blood. before the altar.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:15:6)


**כְּזָוִיּוֹת מִזְבֵּחַ.** שֶׁמְּנַסְּכִין שָׁם יַיִן וְהַיַּיִן צָף עָלָיו כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סֻכָּה (סוכה מט:) לְשׁוֹן שְׂבִיעָה לְשׁוֹן שִׁכְרוּת:


**like the corners of the altar** where wine is libated. The wine would flow upon it, as we learned in tractate Sukkah (49b). This is an expression of satiety, an expression of drunkenness.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:16:1)


**כְּצֹאן עַמּוֹ.** כַּצֹּאן שֶׁהִסִּיעַ מִמִּצְרַיִם לִהְיוֹת לוֹ לְעָם:


**like the flocks of His people** Like the flocks that He caused to travel from Egypt to become His people.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:16:2)


**כִּי אַבְנֵי נֵזֶר.** כִּי הַכֹּהֲנִים בְּנֵי חַשְׁמוֹנָאִים הַמִּתְפָּאֲרִים בְּאַבְנֵי נֵזֶר בְּטוּרֵי חֹשֶׁן וְאֵפוֹד יִהְיוּ מִתְנוֹסְסִים בְּנִסִּים עַל אַדְמָתָן:


**for crown stones are exalted on His land** For the priests, the sons of the Hasmoneans, who boast of the crown stones in the rows of the breast-plate and the ephod, will be exalted with miracles on their land.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:17:1)


**כִּי מַה.** רַב **טוּבוֹ.** הַצָּפוּן שֶׁל אוֹתוֹ הַדּוֹר:


**For how** How great is the goodness laid out for that generation.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:17:2)


**דָּגָן בַּחוּרִים.** הַנּוֹתֵן כֹּחַ לַבַּחוּרִים:


**Corn... young men** Which gives strength to young men.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 9:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/9:17:3)


**וְתִירוֹשׁ.** אֲשֶׁר **יְנוֹבֵב בְּתוּלוֹת** בְּנִיב שִׁיר וְשִׂמְחָה. וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין אֲשֶׁר יְנוֹבֵב וְיִצְמַח בְּקַרְקַע בְּתוּלָה, וְהוּא יַיִן מְשֻׁבָּח:


**and new wine** Which causes maids to speak in song and joy. Others interpret יְנוֹבֵב [as that] which will be fruitful and grow in virgin soil, which is superior wine.



## Chapter 10


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:1:1)


**שַׁאֲלוּ מֵה׳ מָטָר.** אִם תִּצְטָרְכוּ:


**Request rain from the Lord** if you need it.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:1:2)


**חֲזִיזִים.** עֲנָנִים מְבִיאִים מָטָר:


**rain-clouds** Clouds that bring rain


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:1:3)


**לְאִישׁ עֵשֶׂב בַּשָּׂדֶה.** בִּשְׁבִיל אִישׁ אֶחָד וּבִשְׁבִיל עֵשֶׂב אֶחָד הַצָּרִיךְ לְמָטָר (תענית ט:):


**for each person, grass in the field** For one man and for one grass that requires rain.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:2:1)


**כִּי הַתְּרָפִים.** וְהַקּוֹסְמִים חָזוּ שֶׁקֶר וְהַחֲלוֹמוֹת אֲשֶׁר הִטְעוּ אֶת אֲבוֹתֵיכֶם ״לֵאמֹר חָלַמְתִּי״ (ירמיה כג:כה) וְהָיוּ מַחֲזִיקִים יְדֵיהֶם לִמְרֹד בְּהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, דִּבְּרוּ אָוֶן וְהֶבֶל דִּבְּרוּ לֵאמֹר ״שָׁלוֹם וְאֵין שָׁלוֹם״ (ירמיה ו:יד):


**For the teraphim, etc., and the soothsayers envisioned lies, and dreamers** who misled your forefathers, saying, “I dreamt.” They would encourage [your forefathers] to rebel against the Holy One, blessed be He. They spoke futility, and they spoke vanity, saying “peace” - but there was no peace.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:2:2)


**עַל כֵּן נָסְעוּ.** אֲבָל אֲנִי נָבִיא לָהֶם בֶּאֱמֶת:


**Therefore, they traveled.** I, however, am a true prophet for them.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:2:3)


**יַעֲנוּ.** יִתְכַּנְּעוּ, כְּמוֹ ״לֵעָנֹת מִפְּנֵי״ (שמות י:ג) – לְשׁוֹן עֹנִי:


**they were humbled** “to humble yourself before Me.” An expression of humility.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:3:1)


**עַל הָרֹעִים חָרָה אַפִּי.** עַל מַלְכֵי נְבוּכַדְנֶצַּר וְסַנְחֵרִיב (נוסח אחר: מַלְכֵי יָוָן):


**My wrath is kindled against the shepherds** Against the kings of Greece.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:3:2)


**וְעַל הָעַתּוּדִים.** עַל שָׂרֵיהֶם, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְעַל מַלְכַיָּא הֲוָה רוּגְזִי וְעַל שָׁלְטָנַיָּא אֲנָא מְסָעֵר״. וְעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת נִמְשְׁלוּ לַעֲתוּדִים: ״וְהַצָּפִיר הַשָּׂעִיר מֶלֶךְ יָָוָן״ (דניאל ח:כא). וְהָרוֹצֶה לְפָרֵשׁ הָעִנְיָן שֶׁלֹּא בְּמַלְכֵי עוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת יֹאמַר: עַתּוּדִים – לְשׁוֹן שָׂרִים, כְּמוֹ ״עוֹרֵר לְךָ רְפָאִים כָּל עַתּוּדֵי אָרֶץ״ (ישעיה יד:ט):


**upon the goats** Against their princes, and so did Jonathan render it: My wrath was against the kings and upon the rulers I visit. The Greeks were likened to goats in (Dan. 8:21), “And the rough he-goat is the king of Greece.” Whoever wishes to explain the matter [otherwise] (not as referring to the kings of Greece) will say that הָעַתּוּדִים is an expression of princes, as in (Isa. 14:9), “It aroused the giants for you, all the chiefs of the earth.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:3:3)


**כְּסוּס הוֹדוֹ בַּמִּלְחָמָה.** כְּסוּס אֲשֶׁר יֻכַּר הוֹדוֹ בַּמִּלְחָמָה:


**as His majestic horse in battle** As the horse whose majesty is recognized in battle.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:4:1)


**מִמֶּנּוּ פִנָּה.** מֵהֶם מַלְכֵיהֶם, מֵהֶם שָׂרֵיהֶם, מֵהֶם לוֹחֲמֵי מִלְחֲמוֹתָם (חולין נו:):


**Out of them shall come the cornerstone** Out of them shall come their kings; out of them shall come their princes, and out of them shall come those who wage their wars.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:5:1)


**וְהָיוּ כְגִבּוֹרִים בּוֹסִים בְּטִיט חוּצוֹת בַּמִּלְחָמָה.** הֲרֵי מִקְרָא זֶה מְסֹרָס, וְהָיוּ בַּמִּלְחָמָה הוֹלְכִים וְדוֹרְכִים אֲנָשִׁים כְּגִבּוֹרִים הַבּוֹסִים טִיט חוּצוֹת. **בּוֹסִים** – דּוֹרְסִים, כְּמוֹ ״יָבוּס קָמֵינוּ״ (תהילים מד:ו), ״בּוֹסְסוּ אֶת חֶלְקָתִי״ (ירמיהו יב:י):


**And they shall be like mighty men, treading the mire of the streets in battle** This verse is inverted. It should be understood: And they shall be in battle, walking and treading people as mighty men tread the mire of the streets. **treading** as in (Ps. 60:14), “And He shall tread down our enemies,” and (Jer. 12:10) “They have trampled My field.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:5:2)


**וְהוֹבִישׁוּ.** בֵּית יְהוּדָה, אֶת רוֹכְבֵי הַסּוּסִים הַבָּאִים לְהִלָּחֵם בָּם:


**and they shall shame the riders of horses** The house of Judah shall shame the riders of horses who come to wage war with them.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:1)


**וְגִבַּרְתִּי אֶת בֵּית יְהוּדָה.** בְּמִלְחֶמֶת הַיְּוָנִים:


**And I will strengthen the house of Judah** in the war against the Greeks.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:2)


**וְאֶת בֵּית יוֹסֵף אוֹשִׁיעַ.** בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁגָּלוּ שָׁם בַּחֲלַח וּבְחָבוֹר בִּימֵי סַנְחֵרִיב (מלכים ב יח:יא):


**and the house of Joseph I will save** in the place where they were exiled - in Halah and in Habor in the days of Sennacherib.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:3)


**וְהוֹשְׁבוֹתִים.** כְּמוֹ וְהוֹשַׁבְתִּים, לְשׁוֹן יְשִׁיבָה:


**And I will cause them to settle** Like וְהוֹשַּׁבְתִּים, an expression of settling.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:6:4)


**וְהָיוּ כַּאֲשֶׁר לֹא זְנַחְתִּים.** כְּאִלּוּ לֹא זְנַחְתִּים מֵעוֹלָם:


**And they shall be as though I had not forsaken them** As though I had never forsaken them.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:1)


**אֶשְׁרְקָה לָהֶם.** כְּדֶרֶךְ הַשּׁוֹרְקִים לְסִימָן אוֹת לָבֹא אֵלָיו הַטּוֹעֶה בַּדֶּרֶךְ:


**I will whistle to them** in the manner of those who whistle as a sign and a signal for those straying on their way to him.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:2)


**וַאֲקַבְּצֵם.** לְעֵת הַקֵּץ, לֶעָתִיד:


**and I will gather them** at the time of the end in the future.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:3)


**כִּי פְדִיתִים.** כְּשֶׁאֶפְדֵּם:


**for I have redeemed them** When I will redeem them.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:8:4)


**וְרָבוּ.** בַּגּוֹלָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁרָבוּ בְּמִצְרָיִם (שמות א:ז):


**And they shall multiply** in exile as they multiplied in Egypt.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:9:1)


**וְאֶזְרָעֵם.** תְּחִלָּה בָּעַמִּים, כְּאָדָם הַזּוֹרֵעַ סְאָה לְהַכְנִיס כַּמָּה כּוֹרִין (פסחים פז:):


**And I will sow them** first among the peoples, as a person sows a seah to bring in many korim.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:9:2)


**וְחָיוּ אֶת בְּנֵיהֶם.** עִם בְּנֵיהֶם:


**And they shall live with their children** אֶת-בְנֵיהֶם



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:10:1)


**וְלַבְנוֹן.** זֶה בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ (יומא לט:):


**and the Lebanon** This is the Temple.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:10:2)


**וְלֹא יִמָּצֵא לָהֶם.** ״וְלֹא יִסֻפָּק לְהוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**and it shall not suffice for them** Heb. וְלֹא יִמָּצֵא לָהֶם



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:11:1)


**וְעָבַר בַּיָּם צָרָה.** וְתַעֲבֹר צָרָה בְּצוֹר הַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת בְּתוֹךְ הַיָּם, וְהוּא רֹאשׁ לְמַלְכוּת צוֹר:


**And trouble shall pass through the sea** And the trouble shall pass through Tyre, which is situated in the midst of the sea, it is the head of Edom.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:11:2)


**וְהִכָּה.** הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא אֲשֶׁר הוּא הַמַּכֶּה יַכֶּה **בַיָּם גַּלִּים** לְהַשְׁקִיעַ אֶת צוֹר:


**and He shall strike** The Holy One, blessed be He, Who is the One Who strikes **the waves of the sea** to sink Tyre.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 10:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/10:11:3)


**כָּל מְצוּלוֹת יְאוֹר.** הִיא מִצְרָיִם:


**all the depths of the river** That is, Egypt.



## Chapter 11


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:1:1)


**פְּתַח לְבָנוֹן דְּלָתֶיךָ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״פִּתְחוּ עַמְמַיָּא תַּרְעֵיכוֹן״:


**Open your doors, O Lebanon** Jonathan renders: O peoples, open your gates.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:1)


**הֵילֵל בְּרוֹשׁ כִּי נָפַל אֶרֶז.** שֶׁהוּא גָּדוֹל מִן הַבְּרוֹשׁ. הֵילִילוּ הַשִּׁלְטוֹנִים כִּי נָפְלוּ הַמְּלָכִים מֵעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת:


**Wail, O cypress, for the cedar has fallen** [The cedar,] which is bigger than the cypress, [has fallen]. Howl, O rulers, for the kings of the nations have fallen.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:2)


**אַלּוֹנֵי בָשָׁן.** קִישְׁנֵ״שׁ בְּלַעַ״ז:


**Wail, O oaks of the Bashan** Kesnes or chesnes in Old French, chenes in Modern French.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:3)


**כִּי יָרַד.** כִּי נִשְׁבַּר, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ ״וּבָרַד בְּרֶדֶת הַיַּעַר״ (ישעיה לב:יט):


**for... has gone down** For it has been broken. Similar to [this expression] is (Isa. 32:19), “And He shall hail down the breaking of the forest.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:2:4)


**יַעַר הַבָּצִיר.** יַעַר הַמִּבְצָר, ״תְּקוֹף כַּרְכַיָּא״:


**the fortified forest** The fortified forest, the strong walled cities.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:1)


**הָרוֹעִים.** הַמְּלָכִים:


**the shepherds** The kings.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:2)


**אַדַּרְתָּם.** תִּפְאַרְתָּם׃


**for their glory** אַדַּרְתָּם


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:3)


**שַׁאֲגַת כְּפִירִים.** יִבְכּוּ הַשָּׂרִים:


**the roar of the young lions** The princes shall weep.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:3:4)


**כִּי שֻׁדַּד גְּאוֹן הַיַּרְדֵּן.** שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם הַכְּפִירִים וַאֲרָיוֹת. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ פְּתַח לְבָנוֹן דְּלָתֶיךָ (יומא לט:), שֶׁעַל חֻרְבַּן בַּיִת שֵׁנִי נִתְנַבֵּא הַנָּבִיא, וְאַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה קֹדֶם חֻרְבָּן הָיוּ דַּלְתוֹת הַהֵיכָל נִפְתָּחוֹת מֵאֲלֵיהֶן. גָּעַר בָּהֶן רַבִּי יוֹחָנָן בֶּן זַכַּאי, אָמַר לוֹ: הֵיכָל הֵיכָל, עַד מָתַי אַתָּה מְבַעִית אֶת עַצְמְךָ? יוֹדֵעַ אֲנִי שֶׁסּוֹפְךָ לֵחָרֵב, וּכְבָר נִתְנַבֵּא עָלֶיךָ זְכַרְיָה בֶּן עִדּוֹא פְּתַח לְבָנוֹן דְּלָתֶיךָ וְגוֹ׳:


**for the pride of the Jordan has been spoiled** The pride of the Jordan, which is the place of the young lions and the old lions. Our Sages (Yoma 39b) explained “Open your doors, O Lebanon,” as the prophet prophesying about the destruction of the Second Temple; that forty years prior to the destruction, the doors of the Temple proper would open by themselves. Rabban Yochanan ben Zakkai rebuked them. He said, “Temple, how long will you terrify yourself? I know that you will eventually be destroyed. Zechariah the son of Iddo has already prophesied concerning you: ‘Open your doors, O Lebanon, etc.’”



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:4:1)


**רְעֵה אֶת צֹאן הַהֲרֵגָה.** הָכֵן לָהֶם רוֹעִים, כְּלוֹמַר: הִנָּבֵא עַל פַּרְנָסֵיהֶם הָעֲתִידִים לִרְעוֹתָם מֵעַתָּה:


**Tend the flock of slaughter** Prepare shepherds for them; i.e., prophesy concerning their leaders who are destined to lead them from now on.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:4:2)


**צֹאן הַהֲרֵגָה.** יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהֲרָגוּם רוֹעֵיהֶם וַאֲכָלוּם:


**the flock of slaughter** Israel, whose shepherds slew them and devoured them.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:5:1)


**וְלֹא יֶאְשָׁמוּ.** הַבַּבְלִיִּים שֶׁאַגְלֵם לְתוֹכָם, זֶה מוֹכְרָם וְהַקּוֹנֶה הָרוּג, וְאֵין נוֹתֵן לְלִבּוֹ שֶׁיְּהֵא לוֹ אַשְׁמָה בַּדָּבָר. וּמוֹכְרָם מִתְהַלֵּל: **בָּרוּךְ ה׳** שֶׁמְּסָרָם בְּיָדִי, וַהֲרֵינִי עָשִׁיר:


**and not be guilty** The kings of the nations among whom I will exile them; this one sells them, and the buyer slays them, without feeling (lit., “and he does not put to his heart”) that there should be guilt in the matter. The seller boasts: **Blessed be the Lord** Who delivered them into my hand, and behold! I am wealthy.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:5:2)


**וָאֶעְשִׁיר.** וַהֲרֵי אֲנִי עָשִׁיר:


**for I have become wealthy** And behold! I am wealthy.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:1)


**וָאֶרְעֶה אֶת צֹאן הַהֲרֵגָה.** כָּל אֵלֶּה דִּבְרֵי הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַנָּבִיא, וַאֲנִי רָעִיתִי אוֹתָם בַּיָּמִים הַקַּדְמוֹנִים:


**And I tended the flock of slaughter** All these are the words of the Holy One, blessed be He, to the prophet: And I tended them in the early days.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:2)


**לָכֵן עֲנִיֵּי הַצֹּאן.** אֱמֶת עֲנִיֵּי הַצֹּאן הָיוּ כְּשֶׁהִתְחַלְתִּי לִרְעוֹתָם:


**indeed, the poor of the flock** Indeed, they were the poor of the flock when I began to tend them.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:3)


**וָאֶקַּח לִי שְׁנֵי מַקְלוֹת.** לְסוֹף יָמִים חִלַּקְתִּים לִשְׁתֵּי מַמְלָכוֹת בַּעֲוֹנָם:


**and I took for Myself two staffs** At the end of a period of time, I divided them into two kingdoms because of their iniquity.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:4)


**לְאַחַד קָרָאתִי נֹעַם.** יָרָבְעָם אָמַר לְנַהֲלָם לְאַט:


**one I called Pleasantness** Jeroboam promised to lead them gently.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:7:5)


**וּלְאַחַד קָרָאתִי חוֹבְלִים.** רְחַבְעָם אָמַר לָהֶם לְיַסְּרָם בְּעַקְרַבִּים (מלכים א יב:יד). וּלְפִי שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ צֹאן לְנַהֲלָן בְּמַקְלוֹת, קָרָא לְמוֹשְׁלֵיהֶם מַקְלוֹת:


**and one I called Destoyers** Rehoboam told [his kingdom] that he would flog them with scorpions (I Kings 12:11). [Zechariah] calls their rulers staffs because it is customary to lead flocks with staffs.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:8:1)


**וָאַכְחִיד אֶת שְׁלֹשֶׁת הָרוֹעִים בְּיֶרַח אֶחָד.** וְהֵם הִשְׁחִיתוּ דַרְכֵיהֶם עַד שֶׁמְאַסְתִּים וַהֲרַגְתִּים בְּחֹדֶשׁ אֶחָד. כָּל **שְׁלֹשֶׁת הָרוֹעִים** שֶׁהָרַג וְהוּא אֶת כָּל בֵּית אַחְאָב וְאֶת בֵּית אֲחַזְיָה מֶלֶךְ יְהוּדָה וְאֶת אֶחָיו וְאֶת כָּל זֶרַע מַלְכוּת דָּוִד, וַעֲתַלְיָה אִבְּדָה אֶת הַשְּׁאָר, זוּלָתִי יוֹאָשׁ שֶׁנִּטְמַן (מלכים ב יא:א-ג):


**I cut off the three shepherds in one month** They corrupted their ways until I rejected them, and I slew all three shepherds in one month. Jehu slew the entire house of Ahab, and the house of Ahaziah the king of Judah, and his brothers, and all the seed of the kingdom of David; and Athaliah slew the rest, save Joash, who hid (II Kings 10, 11).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:8:2)


**וַתִּקְצַר נַפְשִׁי בָּהֶם.** מְאַסְתִּים. וְכָל לְשׁוֹן קֹצֶר נֶפֶשׁ הוּא דְּבַר צָרָה אוֹ דְּבַר מֵאוּס שֶׁאֵין הַדַּעַת סוֹבַלְתּוֹ, וְקָצַר הַלֵּב וְהַמֵּעַיִם מִלְּהָכִיל; כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֱלִיהוּ אָמַר: ״הֱצִיקַתְנִי רוּחַ בִּטְנִי״ (אִיּוֹב לב:יח):


**I could not tolerate them** “My soul was short with them” I rejected them. And every expression of shortness of soul denotes a distressful or disgusting matter, that a person’s thoughts cannot tolerate. His heart and his reigns are too short to contain it, as Elihu said (Job 32:18), “The spirit of my innards constrains me.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:8:3)


**גַּם נַפְשָׁם בָּחֲלָה בִי.** זִכְרוֹנָם הָיָה רַב בְּמֵעַי וּמְמַלֵּא אֶת רוּחִי וּמֵצִיק אֶת בִּטְנִי. וּבָחֲלָה פֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ לְשׁוֹן גֹּדֶל בְּמַסֶּכֶת נִדָּה (נדה מז.): מָשָׁל מָשְׁלוּ חֲכָמִים בָּאִשָּׁה פַּגָּה בֹּחַל וְצֶמֶל, פַּגָּה עוֹדָהּ תִּינוֹקֶת, בֹּחַל אֵלּוּ יְמֵי הַנְּעוּרִים שֶׁכְּבָר הִיא גְּדוֹלָה. וְהֵבִיאוּ מִקְרָא זֶה רְאָיָה לְדִבְרֵיהֶם:


**moreover, they were too much for Me** Their memory was too big for My innards, and it filled My spirit and constrained My reins. The word בָּחֲלָה was explained by our Sages in tractate Niddah (47a) as an expression of largeness. The Sages depicted the development of a woman with a metaphor: פַּגָּה, unripe figs; בֹּחַל, larger figs, and צֶמֶל, completely ripe figs. She is compared to unripe figs when she is still a child; she is compared to larger figs in the days of her youth, when she is already bigger. They brought this verse as proof of their words.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:9:1)


**וָאֹמַר לֹא אֶרְעֶה אֶתְכֶם.** אָמַרְתִּי בְּאוֹתָן הַיָּמִים אַשְׁלִיכֵם מֵעַל פָּנַי וְיִהְיוּ הֶפְקֵר וּלְבַז:


**And I said, “I will not tend you...”** I said in those days, “I will cast them from before Me,” and they shall be free and subject to spoil.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:10:1)


**וָאֶקַּח אֶת מַקְלִי אֶת נֹעַם.** שָׁבַרְתִּי אֶת כֹּחַ מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּימֵי יְהוֹאָחָז בֶּן יֵהוּא, עַד כִּי אִבְּדָם מֶלֶךְ אֲרָם וַיְשִׂימֵם כֶּעָפָר לָדוּשׁ (מלכים ב יג:ז). וּבִימֵי הוֹשֵׁעַ בֶּן אֵלָה, שֶׁמְּסַרְתִּים בְּיַד סַנְחֵרִיב וְהִגְלָהוּ (שם יז):


**And I took My [first] staff, [called] Pleasantness** I broke the power of the kings of Israel in the days of Jehoahaz the son of Jehu - to the extent that the king of Aram destroyed them and made them like dust to trample (II Kings 13:7) - and in the days of Hoshea the son of Elah, when I delivered them into the hands of Sennacherib and he exiled them (ibid. 17:6).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:10:2)


**לְהָפֵר אֶת בְּרִיתִי אֲשֶׁר כָּרַתִּי אֶת כָּל הָעַמִּים.** לְהַרְאוֹתָם, שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁבָּגְדוּ בִּי הֵפַרְתִּי אֶת בְּרִיתִי **אֲשֶׁר כָּרַתִּי** עִם **כָּל הָעַמִּים** עֲלֵיהֶם שֶׁלֹּא יַזִּיקוּם. שֶׁעַל מְנָת כֵּן נָתַתִּי לָהֶם אֶת הַתּוֹרָה, שֶׁאִם יִשְׁמְרוּהָ, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ חֵרוּת מִן הַמַּלְכֻיּוֹת; שֶׁלֹּא תְּהֵא אֻמָּה וְלָשׁוֹן שׁוֹלֶטֶת בָּהֶם (ערובין נד.). וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ אִם דִּבֵּר הַמִּקְרָא תְּשׁוּעָתָם מִיַּד שׂוֹנֵא בִּלְשׁוֹן כְּרִיתַת בְּרִית אֶת הַשּׂוֹנֵא, שֶׁהֲרֵי מָצִינוּ מִקְרָא דֻּגְמָתוֹ: ״וְכָרַתִּי לָהֶם בְּרִית בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא עִם חַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה וְגוֹמֵר״ (הושע ב:כ):


**to nullify My covenant that I [had] formed with all the peoples** To show them that because they betrayed Me, I nullified My covenant that I formed with all the peoples concerning them, that the [peoples] not harm them. For, on that condition I gave them the Torah, that if they keep it, they will be free from the kingdoms; that no nation or tongue shall rule over them. And do not be surprised if Scripture speaks of their salvation from the hands of the enemy as forming a covenant with the enemy, for we find a similar verse (Hosea 2:20): “And I will make a covenant for them on that day with the beasts of the field, etc.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:11:1)


**וַיֵּדְעוּ.** כֵּן הֵבִינוּ הַצַּדִּיקִים שֶׁבָּהֶם הַשּׁוֹמְרִים אֶת חוּקִּי:


**knew this** The righteous among them who kept My statute understood.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:11:2)


**כִּי דְבַר ה׳ הוּא.** גְּזֵרָה זוֹ כְּבָר דִּבֵּר לָנוּ הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא עַל יְדֵי מֹשֶׁה: ״יוֹלִיךְ ה׳ אֹתְךָ וְאֶת מַלְכְּךָ״ (דברים כח:לו):


**that it was the word of the Lord** This decree the Holy One, blessed be He, already spoke to us through Moses (Deut. 28: 36): “The Lord shall drive you and your king.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:1)


**וָאֹמַר.** אֶל מַלְכֵי יְהוּדָה הַנּוֹתָרִים:


**And I said** to the remaining kings of Judah.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:2)


**אִם טוֹב בְּעֵינֵיכֶם הָבוּ שְׂכָרִי.** קַיְּמוּ מִצְוֹתַי, וְהוּא יִהְיֶה **שְׂכָרִי** עַל כָּל הַטּוֹבָה אֲשֶׁר נָתַתִּי לָכֶם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנּוֹתְנִין שָׂכָר לְרוֹעֵה צֹאן, וְאָשׁוּב וְאֶרְעֶה אֶתְכֶם:


**“If it pleases you, give [Me] My hire...”** Fulfill My commandments, and that will be My payment for all the good that I have given you; as they give hire to a shepherd, I will return and tend you.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:3)


**וְאִם לֹא חֲדָלוּ.** וְגַם אֲנִי לֹא אֵיטִיב לָכֶם. וְכֵן מָצִינוּ שֶׁאָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִיחֶזְקֵאל: ״הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ יִשְׁמַע וְהֶחָדֵל יֶחְדָּל״ (יחזקאל ג:כז):


**and if not, forbear** And I, too, will not do good for you. We find that the Holy One, blessed be He, said similarly to Ezekiel (3:27): “He that hears, let him hear, and he that forbears, let him forbear.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:12:4)


**וַיִּשְׁקְלוּ אֶת שְׂכָרִי שְׁלֹשִׁים כָּסֶף.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וַעֲבַדוּ יָת רְעוּתִי גֻּבְרִין מִקְצָת״ – אֲנָשִׁים טוֹבִים מְעַט הָיוּ בָּהֶם שֶׁעָשׂוּ רְצוֹנִי, כְּגוֹן ״הֶחָרָשׁ וְהַמַּסְגֵּר״ (מלכים ב כד:טז), דָּנִיֵּאל חֲנַנְיָה מִישָׁאֵל וַעֲזַרְיָה, יְחֶזְקֵאל. וּלְשׁוֹן **שְׁלֹשִׁים כָּסֶף** – לֹא יָדַעְתִּי לְכַוֵּן כָּאן אֶלָּא **כֶּסֶף** – לְשׁוֹן חֶמְדָּה. וְאַף רַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּהוּ כֵּן בִּחֻלִּין (חולין צב.), וְהֵבִיאוּ רְאָיָה: ״צְרוֹר הַכֶּסֶף לָקַח בְּיָדוֹ״ (משלי ז:כ). וְאֶת **הַשְּׁלֹשִׁים** פֵּרְשׁוּ, שֶׁאַרְבָּעִים וַחֲמִשָּׁה צַדִּיקִים יֵשׁ בְּכָל דּוֹר וָדוֹר; וְהֵבִיאוּ רְאָיָה: ״וְחֹמֶר שְׂעֹרִים וְלֶתֶךְ שְׂעֹרִים״ (הושע ג:ב) – חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר בְּבָבֶל; וּשְׁלֹשִׁים בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר ״וָאֶקְחָה אֶת שְׁלֹשִׁים הַכֶּסֶף בֵּית ה׳״ – בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. וּמִנְיַן **הַשְּׁלֹשִׁים** – מִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה הוּא (תנחומא מקץ ו), שֶׁהֻבְטַח אַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ בְּכָךְ שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא דּוֹר פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשִׁים צַדִּיקִים. מִנְיָן? ״כֹּה יִהְיֶה זַרְעֶךָ״ (בראשית טו:ה) – ״יִהְיֶה״ בְּגִימַטְרִיָּא שְׁלֹשִׁים:


**And they weighed out My hire, thirty pieces of silver** Jonathan paraphrases: And they performed My will with a few men. There were a few good men among them, such as the craftsmen and the sentries, Daniel, Hananiah, Mishael, Azariah, and Ezekiel. But I do not know how to explain the expression here of thirty pieces of silver exactly, except that כֶּסֶף is an expression of desire. Our Sages, too, explained it this way in Chullin (92a). They brought proof from (Prov. 7:20), “The bundle of the desirable ones He took in His hand.” The thirty they explained in the following manner: There are forty-five righteous men in every generation. They brought proof from (Hosea 3:2), “a חֹמֶר of barley and a לֶתֶךְ of barley” - fifteen righteous in Babylon and thirty in Eretz Israel. It is said: “And I took the thirty pieces of silver, and I cast them into the house of the Lord in Eretz Israel.” The number thirty is explained by the Midrash Aggadah (Cf. Gen. Rabbah 49:3, Pesikta d’Rav Kahana 88a), that our father Abraham was promised that no generation would have fewer than thirty righteous in men, the number of (Gen. 15:8): “So shall your seed be.” The word יִהְיֶה has the numerical value of thirty.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:13:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר ה׳ אֵלַי הַשְׁלִיכֵהוּ אֶל הַיּוֹצֵר.** כְּמוֹ אֶל הָאוֹצָר, שׁוֹמֵר הָאוֹצָר. אָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לַנָּבִיא: כְּתֹב וְהַנַּח אֶת אֵלּוּ, הֵם וְצִדְקָתָם, לִהְיוֹת שָׁמוּר לְסוֹף שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה שֶׁל גָּלוּת בָּבֶל, וְיִבָּנֶה בֵּית הַמִּקְדָּשׁ עַל יְדֵיהֶם. וּמַהוּ הָאוֹצָר? **אֲדַר הַיְקָר** בֵּית מִקְדָּשִׁי, אֲדַר תִּפְאַרְתִּי:


**And the Lord said to me: Cast it to the keeper of the treasury** like הָאוֹצֵר, the keeper of the treasury. Said the Holy One, blessed be He, to the prophet: Write, and leave over these and their righteousness to be preserved for the end of the seventy years of the Babylonian exile. The Temple shall be built by them. Now what is the treasury? [Cf. below] **the stronghold of glory** My Temple, the stronghold of My glory.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:13:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יָקַרְתִּי מֵעֲלֵיהֶם.** אֲשֶׁר הִפְשַׁטְתִּי מֵעֲלֵיהֶם מִהְיוֹת לָהֶם לִיקָר. וּלְשׁוֹן **יָקַרְתִּי** הוּא הֲסָרַת כָּבוֹד, וְהַמֵּ״ם שֶׁל **מֵעֲלֵיהֶם** יוֹכִיחַ. וְהוּא כְּמוֹ ״וְשֵׁרֶשְׁךָ מֵאֶרֶץ חַיִּים״ (תהלים נב:ז), כְּמוֹ ״מְסָעֵף פֹּארָה״ (ישעיהו י:לג). וְדֻגְמַת הַפֵּרוּשׁ שֶׁפֵּרַשְׁתִּי, מֵעֵין תַּרְגּוּם שֶׁל יוֹנָתָן הוּא. וְרָאִיתִי לְשׁוֹנוֹת הַרְבֵּה לְפֵרוּשׁ נְבוּאָה זוֹ, וְאֵינִי יָכוֹל לְכַוְּנָן:


**of which I stripped them** of which I stripped them so that they should no longer have glory. The expression יָקַרְתִּי means the removal of glory; the “mem” in מֵעֲלֵיהֶם proves it. It is like (Ps. 52:7), “and He shall uproot you from the land of the living”; and like (Isa. 10:33), “lops off the branches.” My explanation is similar to Jonathan’s translation. I have seen many variant versions of the explanation of this prophecy, but I cannot reconcile those with the text.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:14:1)


**וָאֶגְדַּע אֶת מַקְלִי הַשֵּׁנִי.** הִגְלֵיתִי אֶת צִדְקִיָּהוּ:


**And I cut off My second staff** I exiled Zedekiah.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:14:2)


**אֶת הַחוֹבְלִים.** אֶת רִשְׁעֵי דּוֹרוֹ, כִּי הוּא צַדִּיק וְדוֹרוֹ רְשָׁעִים (סנהדרין קג.):


**the destroyers** The wicked of his generation, for he was righteous, but his generation was wicked.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:14:3)


**לְהָפֵר אֶת הָאַחֲוָה.** שֶׁנִּתְאַחֲווּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וּבִנְיָמִן וְנִדְבְּקוּ בְּתוֹעֲבוֹת מַלְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**to nullify the brotherhood** that the Judahites and the Benjamites were joined in brorherhood, and that they adhered to the abominations of the kings of Israel.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:15:1)


**עוֹד קַח לְךָ וְגוֹ׳.** וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ לְמַעְלָה **רְעֵה צֹאן הַהֲרֵגָה**, סִימָן הוּא שֶׁאֲנִי עָתִיד לִמְסֹר דּוֹר חֻרְבַּן בַּיִת שֵׁנִי זֶה בְּיַד טִיטוּס:


**Take for yourself, etc.** This is what He said to him above: Tend the flock of slaughter (verse 4). This is a sign that I am destined to deliver the generation of the destruction of this Second Temple into the hands of Esau.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:1)


**הַנִּכְחָדוֹת.** הָאוֹבְדוֹת לֹא יִפְקֹד הָרוֹעֶה לְבַקְּשָׁם:


**Those that are cut off** The shepherd will not remember to seek the lost ones.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:2)


**הַנַּעַר לֹא יְבַקֵּשׁ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן ״דְּאִטַּלְטְלוּ לָא יִתְבַּע״. הַנַּעַר – הַשּׁוֹטוֹת שֶׁאֵינָן יוֹדְעוֹת לָבוֹא אֶל הַדִּיר:


**the foolish ones he shall not seek** Jonathan renders: Those that wandered off he shall not seek. The word הַנַּעַר means the foolish ones that do not know to enter the fold.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:3)


**הַנִּצָּבָה.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ כֹּחַ קְצָת לַעֲמֹד עַל רַגְלֶיהָ, וּצְרִיכָה סִיּוּעַ לְנַהֲלָהּ לְאַט. וּמְנַחֵם פֵּרֵשׁ וְנִצָּבָה לְשׁוֹן ״לַצְבּוֹת בֶּטֶן״ (במדבר ה:כב):


**the one that can stand** The one that has a little strength to stand on its feet and requires help, [the one that needs] to be led slowly. Menahem (Machbereth p. 148) explained: and the swollen one, as in (Num. 5:22), “to cause the belly to swell.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:4)


**לֹא יְכַלְכֵּל.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לָא יְסוֹבַר״, וְדֶרֶךְ הָרוֹעֶה בְּחֵיקוֹ יִשָּׂא (יְשַׁעְיָהוּ מ:יא):


**he shall not bear** Jonathan renders. he shall not bear. It is the custom of the shepherd to carry the lambs in his bosom.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:5)


**וּבְשַׂר הַבְּרִיאָה יֹאכַל.** אֶת הָעֲשִׁירִים יִגְמֹר מָמוֹנָן:


**And the flesh of the fat one he shall eat** He will finish the money of the wealthy.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:16:6)


**וּפַרְסֵיהֶן יְפָרֵק.** עַד כָּלָה. וְדֻגְמַת זוֹ אָמַר דָּנִיֵּאל (דניאל ז:ז): ״אָכְלָה וּמַדֱּקָה וּשְׁאָרָא בְּרַגְלַהּ רָפְסָה״:


**and their hoofs he shall break** until they are finished. Daniel (7:7) stated a figure similar to this: “It devoured and broke in pieces, and the residue it trampled with its foot.”



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:1)


**הוֹי.** יֵשׁ לִצְעוֹק עַל זֶה:


**Ho** There is reason to cry out concerning this.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:2)


**רוֹעֵי הָאֱלִיל.** רוֹעֵי שֶׁל אַיִן, אֱלִיל לְשׁוֹן אַל, אֵינוֹ רוֹעֶה. וְכֵן רֹפְאֵי אֱלִיל (איוב יג:ד). **רוֹעֵי הָאֱלִיל** יוֹ״ד יְתֵרָה בִּמְקוֹם הֵ״א, כְּמוֹ **וְעֹזְבֵי הַצֹּאן** (כאן), ״שֹׁכְנִי סְנֶה״ (דְּבָרִים לג:טז), ״שֹׁכְנִי לְבָדָד״ (מִיכָה ז:יד):


**worthless shepherd** a shepherd of nought. אֶלִיל, is an expression of אַל, naught; he is not a shepherd. [There is also] (in Job 13:4) “worthless physicians.” רֹעִי The “yud” is superfluous; it is instead of a “he,” as “who abandons the flock”; (Deut. 33:16) who dwells (שׁכְנִי) in the bramble”; and (Micha 7:14) “who dwells alone.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:3)


**עוֹזְבִי הַצֹּאן.** נוֹתְנָן הֶפְקֵר לְיַד כָּל הַבָּא לְהַכּוֹת וְלַהֲרֹג וְלָבֹז:


**who abandons the flock** Who leaves them free for anyone who comes to beat, to slay, and to spoil.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:4)


**חֶרֶב עַל זְרוֹעוֹ וְעַל עֵין יְמִינוֹ.** וְהוּא הָרוֹעֶה סַכִּין שֶׁל שְׁחִיטָה נוֹשֵׂא בְּיָדוֹ לִשְׁחוֹט אֶת הַשְּׁמֵנָה וְאֶת הַבְּרִיאָה, שֶׁנָּתַן **עֵין יְמִינוֹ** בָּהֶם לָדַעַת מִי הָעֲשִׁירִים לָבֹז וְלִגְמוֹר נִכְסֵיהֶם:


**A sword is on his arm and his right eye** And that shepherd carries a slaughtering knife in his hand to slaughter the fat and the healthy, upon which he cast his right eye to know who are the wealthy, to spoil and finish their property.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 11:17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/11:17:5)


**זְרוֹעוֹ יָבוֹשׁ תִּיבָשׁ.** לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא: ״וְנָתַתִּי נִקְמָתִי וְגוֹמֵר״ (יחזקאל כח:יד). וְהַפּוֹתְרִים פּוֹתְרִים אוֹתוֹ בְּצִדְקִיָּהוּ, עַל שֵׁם ״וְאֶת עֵינֵי צִדְקִיָּהוּ עִוֵּר״ (ירמיה כב:יא). וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְכַוֵּן ״הִנְנִי מֵקִים רָעָה בָּאָרֶץ״ בְּצִדְקִיָּהוּ, שֶׁכְּבָר עָבַר לוֹ שִׁבְעִים שָׁנָה:


**his arm shall wither** In the future (as in Ezek. 25:14), “I will wreak My vengeance, etc.” The commentators, however, interpreted this as a reference to Zedekiah, an allusion to (II Kings 25:7), “and they blinded Zedekiah’s eyes.” But it is impossible to reconcile “Behold! I am setting up a shepherd in the land” as referring to Zedekiah, for seventy years had already passed after him.



## Chapter 12


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:1:1)


**מַשָּׂא דְבַר ה׳.** הוּא גְּמַר זֶה הַדָּבָר שֶׁהִתְחִיל לְדַבֵּר בְּפֻרְעָנוּתָן שֶׁל אוֹכְלֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַל בָּבֶל וְכַיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ:


**The prophecy of the word of the Lord** This is the completion of the word that he began to speak about the retribution of those who devour Israel - Esau and his ilk.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:2:1)


**סַף רַעַל.** כְּלִי תַּרְעֵלָה, כְּמוֹ ״בַּדָּם אֲשֶׁר בַּסַּף״ (שמות יב:כב). **רַעַל** לְשׁוֹן שִׁמָּמוֹן, אֹטֶם הַלֵּב וְהָאֵבָרִים שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם כֹּחַ, כְּאָדָם הַמְעֻטָּף בְּשִׂמְלָה, כְּמוֹ (נחום ב:ד) ״הַבְּרוֹשִׁים הָרְעָלוּ״, (ישעיה ג:יט) ״הַשֵּׁרוֹת וְהָרְעָלוֹת״. בִּלְשׁוֹן מִשְׁנָה (שבת ו,ו) מָצִינוּ מְדִיּוֹת רְעוּלוֹת, אונבלופמנט״א בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a cup of weakness** a vessel of weakness, as in (Exod. 12:22), “in the blood that is in the cup.” **weakness** an expression of bewilderment, [it means] the clogging of the heart and the limbs, for, like man enwrapped in a garment, they have no strength, as in (Nahum 2:4), “And the cypress trees were enwrapped” ; and (Isa. 3:19) “And the bracelets and the veils.” In the language of the Mishnah (Shabbath 6:6) we find, “Arabian women may go out veiled.” It is involopiment in O.F.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:2:2)


**וְגַם עַל יְהוּדָה.** יַטִּילוּ הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת אֲשֶׁר יִהְיוּ בַּמָּצוֹר **עַל יְרוּשָׁלִַם**, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בֵית דָּוִד מִבִּפְנִים וְעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת יָצוּרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם, וְיָבֹאוּ גַם בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה עַל כָּרְחָם לָצוּר **עַל יְרוּשָׁלִַם.** כֵּן תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן:


**and also on Judah** the nations that will be in siege against Jerusalem will impose upon Judah, for the house of David will be inside and the gentiles will besiege them. And also, the children of Judah will come, against their will, to besiege Jerusalem. So did Jonathan render it.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:3:1)


**אֶבֶן מַעֲמָסָה.** אֶבֶן מַשָּׂאוֹי הַמֻּטֶּלֶת עַל בְּנֵי אָדָם מִפִּי הַמּוֹשֵׁל לָשֵׂאתָהּ וּלְעָמְסָהּ עַל כְּתֵפַיִם, וְהִיא כְּבֵדָה מְאֹד עַד אֲשֶׁר **כָּל עֹמְסֶיהָ שָׂרוֹט יִשָּׂרֵטוּ**:


**a stone of burden** A stone of burden placed upon men by order of the ruler, to carry it and to load it on their shoulders. It is very heavy, to the extent that all who bear it become gashed.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:4:1)


**אַכֶּה כָל סוּס.** הַצּוֹבְאִים שָׁם **בַּשִּׁגָּעוֹן** וְאֶת רֹכְבֵיהֶם **בָּעִוָּרוֹן**, אֲבָל **עַל בֵּית יְהוּדָה** שֶׁהֵם עַל כָּרְחָם מִן הַצּוֹבְאִים **אֶפְקַח עֵינַי** לְהָגֵן עֲלֵיהֶם מִן הַמַּכָּה הַזֹּאת:


**I will smite every horse** of those gathered there with bewilderment and blindness, and their riders with madness (Shem Ephraim). But upon the [members of the] house of Judah, who are among those gathered against their will, I will open My eyes to protect them from this plague.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:5:1)


**וְאָמְרוּ אַלֻּפֵי יְהוּדָה בְּלִבָּם.** כְּשֶׁיִּרְאוּ שְׁאָר הַצּוֹבְאִים נִגָּפִים בְּמַגֵּפָה זוֹ וְהֵם נִצּוֹלִים:


**And the princes of Judah shall say to themselves** when they see that the others who were gathered are plagued with this plague, and they are saved.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:5:2)


**אַמְצָה לִי יוֹשֵׁב יְרוּשָׁלִַם וְגוֹ׳.** יוֹשְׁבֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם שֶׁיּוֹדְעִים שֶׁעַל כָּרְחֵנוּ אָנוּ בָּאִים עֲלֵיהֶם, אִמְּצוּ לְעֶזְרָתִי בִּתְפִלָּתָן בַּה׳ אֱלֹהֵיהֶם:


**The inhabitants of Jerusalem were my strength, etc.** The inhabitants of Jerusalem, who know that we came upon them against our will, strengthened my help with their prayer by the Lord, their God.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:6:1)


**אָשִׂים אֶת אַלֻּפֵי יְהוּדָה.** יָשׁוּבוּ וְיִלָּחֲמוּ עִם אוֹתָם שֶׁהֱבִיאוּם שָׁם:


**I will make the princes of Judah** They will return and wage war with those who brought them there.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:6:2)


**כְּכִיּוֹר אֵשׁ.** כִּמְדוּרוֹת אֵשׁ גְּדוֹלָה. וְכִיּוֹר לְשׁוֹן כִּירָה (ויקרא יא:לה), וְכָךְ חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם:


**as a fiery stove** Like a great bonfire. The word כִּיוֹר is an expression of כִּירָה, a stove. So did Menahem (Machbereth, p. 109) classify it.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:6:3)


**תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** בִּמְקוֹמָהּ:


**in its place** lit. under it.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:7:1)


**וְהוֹשִׁיעַ ה׳ אֶת אָהֳלֵי יְהוּדָה בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה.** בְּטֶרֶם יָבוֹאוּ בְּנֵי יְרוּשָׁלַיִם לָצֵאת מִן הָעִיר, תִּהְיֶה לָהֶם תְּשׁוּעָה לָשׁוּב אֶל אָהֳלֵיהֶם וְאֶל בֵּיתָם. וְאַחַר כָּךְ תָּבֹא תְּשׁוּעָה לִבְנֵי הָעִיר, הִיא הַתְּשׁוּעָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בָּהּ ״וְיָצָא ה׳ וְנִלְחַם״ (זכריה יד:ג):


**And the Lord shall first save the tents of Judah** Before the inhabitants of Jerusalem come out of the city, they shall have a salvation - to return to their tents and to their homes - and afterwards the salvation shall come to the inhabitants of the city. That is the salvation concerning which it is stated (14:3): “And the Lord shall go forth and wage war.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:7:2)


**לְמַעַן לֹא תִגְדַּל.** לְהִתְפָּאֵר עֲלֵיהֶם וְלוֹמַר: לֹא נִצַּלְתֶּם אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבִילֵנוּ:


**so that the boasting of, etc., shall not increase** to boast over them and to say, “You were saved only because of us.”



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:8:1)


**הַנִּכְשָׁל בָּהֶם.** הַחַלָּשׁ שֶׁבָּהֶם:


**and the weakest among them** Heb. הַנִּכְשָׁל


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:8:2)


**כֵּאלֹהִים.** ״כְּרַבְרְבִין״ (תרגום יונתן), כְּמַלְאָכִים:


**like angels** Jonathan renders: like great ones, like angels.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:8:3)


**כְּמַלְאַךְ ה׳ לִפְנֵיהֶם.** ״יִצְלְחוּן כְּמַלְאֲכָא דַה׳ קֳדָמֵיהוֹן״ (תרגום יונתן):


**like the angel of the Lord before them** They will prosper like the angel of the Lord before them.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:9:1)


**אֲבַקֵּשׁ לְהַשְׁמִיד.** אֲבַקֵּשׁ בְּהַשְׁמָדָתָם וַאֲדַקְדֵּק בַּעֲוֹנָם. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּרְשׁוּ לִזְכוּת (עבודה זרה ד.): אֲבַקֵּשׁ בְּנוֹגְנֵי שֶׁלָּהֶם; אִם יֵשׁ לָהֶם זְכוּת – אֲנִיחֵם, וְאִם לָאו – אַשְׁמִידֵם:


**I will seek to destroy** I will seek their destruction, and I will be exact in judging their iniquity. And our Sages (Avodah Zarah 4a) explained this verse as concerning merit. I will search in the books of their deeds; if they have merit, I will spare them; otherwise, I will destroy them.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:1)


**רוּחַ חֵן וְתַחֲנוּנִים.** שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה עַל רוּחָם לְהִתְחַנֵּן לְפָנַי, וְהֵם יִהְיוּ לְחֵן בְּעֵינַי:


**a spirit of grace and supplications** That it should come into their mind to supplicate Me, and they will be in My good graces.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:2)


**רוּחַ.** טָלַנְ״ט בְּלַעַ״ז:


**a spirit** Talant in Old French, a desire.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:3)


**וְהִבִּיטוּ אֵלַי אֵת אֲשֶׁר דָּקָרוּ.** תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וְיִבְעוֹן מִן קֳדָמַי עַל דִּי אִיטַלְטְלוּ״:


**they shall look to Me because of those who have been thrust through** Jonathan renders: And they shall supplicate Me because of their wanderings.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:4)


**וְהִבִּיטוּ.** לְהִתְאוֹנֵן עַל אֲשֶׁר דָּקְרוּ בָּהֶם הָאֻמּוֹת וְהָרְגוּ בָּהֶם בַּגָּלוּת:


**And they shall look to Me.** to complain about those of them whom the nations thrust through and slew during their exile.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:5)


**וְסָפְדוּ עָלָיו.** עַל אוֹתוֹ הֶרֶג:


**and they shall mourn over it** Over that slaughter.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:10:6)


**כְּמִסְפֵּד עַל הַיָּחִיד.** כַּאֲשֶׁר יִסְפֹּד אִישׁ עַל בֵּן יְחִידוֹ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ עַל מָשִׁיחַ בֶּן יוֹסֵף שֶׁנֶּהֱרַג בִּמְסֶכֶת סֻכָּה (סוכה נב.):


**as one mourns over an only son** As a man mourns over his only son. And our Sages expounded this in tractate Sukkah (52a) as referring to the Messiah, son of Joseph, who was slain.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:11:1)


**כְּמִסְפַּד הֲדַדְרִמּוֹן בְּבִקְעַת מְגִדּוֹן.** אֵין עִנְיַן הֲדַדְרִימּוֹן בְּבִקְעַת מְגִדּוֹן, אֶלָּא שְׁנֵי מִסְפָּדוֹת הֵן: ״כְּמִסְפְּדָא דְאַחְאָב בַּר עָמְרִי דְּקָטַל יָתֵיהּ הֲדַדְרִמּוֹן בַּר טַבְרִימוֹן ״בְּרָמוֹת גִּלְעָד, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (מלכים א כב:לו) ״וַיַּעֲבֹר הָרִנָּה בַּמַּחֲנֶה״, וְהוּא הֶסְפֵּד. ״וּכְמִסְפְּדָא דְיֹאשִׁיָּהוּ בַּר אָמוֹן דְּקָטַל יָתֵיהּ פַּרְעֹה חֲגִירָא בְּבִקְעַת מְגִדּוֹן״, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שָׁם (דברי הימים ב לה:כה) ״וַיְקוֹנֵן יִרְמְיָהוּ וְגוֹ׳ וַיֹּאמְרוּ כָל הַשָּׂרִים וְהַשָּׁרוֹת בְּקִינוֹתֵיהֶם וְגוֹ׳״:


**like the mourning of Hadadrimmon in the Valley of Megiddon** Hadadrimmon has no connection to the Valley of Megiddon. These are, rather, two cases of mourning. [The first is] like the mourning of Ahab the son of Omri, who was slain by Hadadrimmon the son of Tabrimmon in Ramoth Gilead, as it is stated (I Kings 22:36): “A cry passed through the camp.” That is the mourning [of Ahab. The second case is] like the mourning of Josiah the son of Amon, who was slain by Pharoah the lame in the Valley of Megiddon, as it is stated (II Chron. 35:25): “And Jeremiah lamented for Josiah, and all the singing men and singing women spoke in their laments, etc.”



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:12:1)


**בֵּית נָתָן.** הַנָּבִיא. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים: נָתָן בֶּן דָּוִד, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״שַׁמּוּעַ וְשׁוֹבָב וְנָתָן וּשְׁלֹמֹה״ (שמואל ב ה:יד):


**the house of Nathan** the prophet. Some say that it refers to Nathan the son of David, as it is said (II Sam. 5:14): “Shammua and Shobab, and Nathan and Solomon.”



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:13:1)


**בֵּית לֵוִי.** הַכֹּהֲנִים וְהַלְוִיִּם:


**the house of Levi** The priests and the Levites.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:13:2)


**מִשְׁפַּחַת הַשִּׁמְעִי.** שַׁמּוּעַ בֶּן דָּוִד (שמואל ב ה:יד), בִּתְחִלָּה כָּלַל הַכָּתוּב מִשְׁפְּחוֹת בֵּית דָּוִד וְאַחַר כָּךְ פֵּרַט כָּל אַחַת וְאַחַת:


**the family of the Shimeites** Shammua the son of David. Scripture first makes a generality about the house of David, and then it specifies each one.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:14:1)


**כָּל הַמִּשְׁפָּחוֹת הַנִּשְׁאָרוֹת.** לְבֵית דָּוִד:


**All the remaining families** of the house of David.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 12:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/12:14:2)


**וּנְשֵׁיהֶם לְבָד.** מִשּׁוּם צְנִיעוּת (סוכה נא:-נב.). וְלָמָּה מִשְׁפְּחוֹת הַכְּהוּנָּה וְהַמַּמְלָכוּת? כְּדֵי לְהַחֲזִיר לָהֶם כִּתְרֵיהֶם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּרֹאשׁ הַסֵּפֶר ״אֵלֶּה שְׁנֵי בְנֵי הַיִּצְהָר הָעֹמְדִים עַל אֲדוֹן כָּל הָאָרֶץ״ (זכריה ד:יד):


**and their wives apart** because of decency. Now why were the families of the priesthood and the kingdom [mentioned]? In order to restore their crowns to them, as it is stated in the beginning of the Book (4:14): “These are the two anointed ones who stand before the Lord of all the earth.”



## Chapter 13


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:1:1)


**לְחַטָּאת וּלְנִדָּה.** לְאַדְיוּתָא, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״אֶשְׁבּוֹק לְחוֹבֵיהוֹן כְּמָא דְמִדְכָּן בְּמֵי אַדְיוּתָא וּבְקִטְם תּוֹרְתָא דְחַטָּאתָא״ – בְּאֵפֶר חַטָּאת:


**for purification and for sprinkling** and for sprinkling. So did Jonathan paraphrase: I will forgive their sins as they become purified with sprinkling water and with the ashes of the cow of purification; i.e., with the ashes of purification.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:2:1)


**וְגַם אֶת הַנְּבִיאִים.** ״נְבִיאֵי שִׁקְרָא״:


**And also the prophets** The false prophets.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:2:2)


**רוּחַ הַטֻּמְאָה.** יֵצֶר הָרָע:


**and the spirit of contamination** The evil inclination.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:4:1)


**אַדֶּרֶת שֵׂעָר.** כֵּן דֶּרֶךְ מְסִיתִים וּמַדִּיחִים, מִתְעַטְּפִים בְּטַלִּיתָם כְּאִלּוּ הֵם אֲנָשִׁים צַדִּיקִים, לְמַעַן יְקֻבְּלוּ כַּחֲשֵׁיהֶם:


**a hairy mantle** So is the custom of those who entice or draw away [the people], to enwrap themselves with mantles as though they are righteous men in order that their lies be accepted.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:5:1)


**כִּי אָדָם הִקְנַנִי.** פָּתַר בּוֹ מְנַחֵם לְשׁוֹן מִקְנֵה צֹאן וּבָקָר (בראשית כו:יד), כִּי אֲנִי כִּשְׁאָר כָּל אָדָם שֶׁאֵינוֹ עָסוּק בִּנְבוּאוֹת, הִפְקִידַנִי מִנְּעוּרַי לִשְׁמֹר אֶת מִקְנֵהוּ:


**for a man entrusted me with his cattle** Menahem (Machbereth p. 156, second definition.) interpreted as an expression of “livestock of flocks and livestock of cattle” (Gen. 26:14). He entrusted me from my youth to watch his livestock.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:6:1)


**בֵּין יָדֶיךָ.** בֵּין כְּתֵפֶיךָ, שָׁם מַלְקִין עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵירָה (מכות כב.):


**And one will say to him, “What are these wounds between your hands?”** Between your shoulders. There they flog the sinners.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:6:2)


**בֵּית מְאַהֲבָי.** אֲשֶׁר אֲהָָבוּנִי וְהוֹכִיחוּנִי לַדֶּרֶךְ הַיְשָׁרָה:


**in the house of my friends** who loved me and reproved me to the straight path.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:1)


**חֶרֶב עוּרִי עַל רֹעִי.** עַל אוֹתוֹ שֶׁהִפְקַדְתִּיו עַל צֹאן גָּלוּתִי:


**O sword, awaken against My shepherd** Against the one whom I appointed over the flock of My exile.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:2)


**וְעַל גֶּבֶר עֲמִיתִי.** שֶׁחִבַּרְתִּיו אֵלַי לִשְׁמֹר צֹאנִי כָּמוֹנִי:


**and against the man who is associated with Me** whom I associated with Me to watch My flocks as I do.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:3)


**הַךְ אֶת הָרוֹעֶה.** אֶת מֶלֶךְ רוֹמִי הָרְשָׁעָה:


**Smite the shepherd** The king of the border of wickedness.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:4)


**וַתְּפוּצֶינָה הַצֹּאן.** שֶׁכְּבוּשָׁה תַּחַת יָדוֹ:


**and the flock shall scatter** The flock subjected to his rule; lit. pressed down under his hand.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:5)


**וַהֲשִׁיבוֹתִי יָדִי.** וְאֶשְׁנֶה מַכּוֹתַי שֵׁנִית:


**and I will return My hand** And I will repeat My blows a second time.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:7:6)


**עַל הַצּוֹעֲרִים.** עַל הַשִּׁלְטוֹנִים הַצְּעִירִים מִן הַמְּלָכִים. וְיוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם וַתְּפוּצֶינָה הַצֹּאן ״וְיִתְבַּדְּרוּן שִׁלְטוֹנַיָּא״, וְתִרְגֵּם עַל הַצּוֹעֲרִים מְמֻנִּים שֶׁלָּהֶם הַשְּׁנִיִּים לָהֶם, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן הַמִּקְרָא הַזֶּה בָּאֻמּוֹת ״חַרְבָּא אִתְגְּלִי עַל מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל וְעַל שִׁלְטוֹנַיָּא חַבְרֵיהּ דִּכְוָתֵיהּ דְּדָמֵי לֵיהּ אֲמַר ה׳ צְבָאוֹת קְטוֹל יַת מַלְכָּא דְבָבֶל וְיִתְבַּדְּרוּן שִׁלְטוֹנַיָּא״. וְאֵין לְשׁוֹן הַתַּרְגּוּם הַזֶּה נוֹפֵל עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל, שֶׁלֹּא מָצִינוּ בְּתַרְגּוּם וּבְמִקְרָא שִׁלְטוֹנִין בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל אֶלָּא מֶלֶךְ וְשָׂרִים וְתַרְגּוּם שֶׁלָּהֶן ״רַבְרְבִין״:


**upon the little ones** Upon the rulers who are smaller than the kings. And Jonathan renders: **and the flock shall scatter** And the rulers shall scatter, and he renders: **upon the little ones** Their officers, who are second to them. And so did Jonathan render this verse as referring to the nations: O sword, reveal yourself against the king and against the ruler, his colleague who is like him, who resembles him, said the Lord of Hosts. Slay the king, and the rulers shall scatter. This wording of the Targum does not apply to Israel, for we do not find in the Targum or in the Scriptures [any words] but “a king and princes” [when referring to Israel], and the Aramaic translation thereof is רַבְרְבִין, great ones.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:8:1)


**וְהַשְּׁלִישִׁית יִוָּתֶר בָּהּ.** יִתְגַּיְּרוּ וְיִחְיוּ:


**and the third shall remain therein** They shall proselytize and live.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:9:1)
