**וְהֵבֵאתִי אֶת הַשְּׁלִישִׁית בָּאֵשׁ.** ״וְאָעֵיל יָת תְּלִיתָא בְּעָקָא בְּכוּר דְּאֶשָּׁא״, שֶׁיִּסְבְּלוּ וְיִשְׂאוּ מִקְצָת הַגֵּרִים מֵהָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָבִים וּמַזָּלוֹת בְּצָרוֹת חֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל מָשִׁיחַ וּבְמִלְחֲמוֹת גּוֹג וּמָגוֹג עִם יִשְׂרָאֵל, וּמִשָּׁם יִבָּחֲנוּ כִּי גֵרֵי אֱמֶת הֵם. כִּי הַרְבֵּה מִן הַמִּתְיַהֲדִים תְּחִלָּה יָשׁוּבוּ לְסוּרָם וְיִתְחַבְּרוּ עִם גּוֹג, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ בָּאַגָּדָה (עבודה זרה ג:):


**And I will bring the third in fire** And I will bring the third in straits in the smelting-pot of fire (Jonathan), so that some of the proselytes should bear the birth pang of the Messiah and the war of Gog and Magog with Israel. From there they shall be tested [as to] whether [or not] they are true proselytes. Then, many of those who previously converted to Judaism shall return to their pre- conversion customs and join Gog. We find [this idea] in the Aggadah (Avodah Zarah 3b; Midrash Psalms 2:5, footnote 29).


###### Rashi on Zechariah 13:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/13:9:2)


**וְהוּא יֹאמַר ה׳.** כְּשֶׁיִּתְפַּלֵּל, יִתְפַּלֵּל לַה׳, וְלֹא לֵאלֹהָיו הָרִאשׁוֹן:


**and he shall say, “The Lord...”** When he prays, he will pray to the Lord and not to his previous deity.



## Chapter 14


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:1:1)


**הִנֵּה יוֹם וְגוֹ׳.** חָבִיב לְהַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


**Behold! A day of the Lord is coming** A day dear to the Lord.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:1:2)


**וְחֻלַּק שְׁלָלֵךְ בְּקִרְבֵּךְ.** שָׁלָל שֶׁתִּשְׁלְלִי מֵאוֹיְבִים יְחַלְּקוּ יוֹשְׁבַיִךְ **בְּקִרְבֵּךְ.** וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״וִיפַלְּגוּן בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל נִכְסֵי עַמְמַיָּא בְּגַוִּיךְ יְרוּשְׁלֵם״:


**and your plunder shall be shared within you** Those dwelling within you shall share the plunder that you shall plunder [from] the enemies. And so does Jonathan render: And the house of Israel shall share the wealth of the peoples in your midst, O Jerusalem.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:2:1)


**וְיֶתֶר הָעָם לֹא יִכָּרֵת.** לֹא יִגְלוּ כֻּלָּם. וְלָמָּה יַנִּיחֵם לְהַגְלוֹת חֶצְיָם וְלָשֹׁסוֹת הַבָּתִּים? כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא לָהֶם פִּתְחוֹן פֶּה לֵאמֹר: לֹא לַמִּלְחָמָה בָּאנוּ, כִּי אִם לְהִשְׁתַּחֲווֹת בָּאנוּ:


**and the rest of the people** Not all of them will be exiled. Now, why will He permit them [i.e., the gentile nations] to exile half of them and to plunder the houses? So that they should not have an excuse, saying, “We have not come for war, but we have come to prostrate ourselves.”



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:3:1)


**כְּיוֹם הִלָּחֲמוֹ בְּיוֹם קְרָב.** ״כְּיוֹם אֲגָחוּתֵיהּ קְרָב עַל יַמָּא דְּסוּף״:


**like the day he waged war on the day of battle** Like the day He waged war on the Red Sea.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:4:1)


**מֵחֶצְיוֹ.** מֵאֶמְצָעִיתוֹ:


**in the midst thereof** lit., from its half


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:4:2)


**מִזְרָחָה וָיָמָּה.** מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב, כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּסַיֵּם וְהוֹלֵךְ:


**toward the east and toward the west** From east to west, as he goes on and concludes.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:4:3)


**וּמָשׁ חֲצִי הָהָר צָפוֹנָה וְגוֹ׳.** חֶצְיוֹ הַצְּפוֹנִי יָמוּשׁ מִמְּקוֹמוֹ וְיִמָּשֵׁךְ לְצַד צָפוֹן, וְכֵן מָצִינוּ חֶצְיוֹ נֶגְבָּה לְצַד דָּרוֹם. וְיִהְיֶה הַגַּיְא בֵּינֵיהֶם, רֹאשׁוֹ לְמִזְרָח וְסוֹפוֹ לְמַעֲרָב:


**and half the mountain shall move to the north, etc.** The northern half shall move from its place and draw toward the north, and so... **and half of it to the south** Toward the south; and the valley between them will have its beginning to the east and its end to the west.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:1)


**וְנִסְתַּם גֵּיא הָרַי.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״וְיִסְתַּתֵּם גֵּיא הָרַי״. לְפִי שֶׁ״יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הָרִים סָבִיב לָהּ״ (תהלים קכה:ב), וְיֵשׁ גַּיְא בֵּין הַר שֶׁבַּצָּפוֹן וּבֵין הַר הַזֵּיתִים, וְכֵן בַּדָּרוֹם. וּכְשֶׁיִּמָּשֵׁךְ חֲצִי הַר הַזֵּיתִים אֵצֶל הַר שֶׁבִּצְפוֹנוֹ, יִסָּתֵם הַגַּיְא שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶם, וְכֵן בַּדָּרוֹם:


**And you shall flee to the valley of the mountains** Jonathan renders. And the valley of the mountains shall be stopped up. Since Jerusalem is surrounded by mountains, and there is a valley between the mountains in the north and the Mount of Olives, and [this is also] so in the south, when half of the Mount of Olives draws toward the mountain in the north, the valley between them will be stopped up. [This will also be] so in the south.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:2)


**כִּי יַגִּיעַ.** גֹּבַהּ גַּיְא שֶׁהָיָה בֵּין הֶהָרִים:


**shall reach** The height of the valley that will be between the mountains.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:3)


**אֶל אָצַל.** אֶל גֹּבַהּ רָאשֵׁי הֶהָרִים שֶׁמִּכָּאן וּמִכָּאן. וְכֵן חִבְּרוֹ מְנַחֵם (ישעיהו מא:ט) ״וּמֵאֲצִילֶיהָ קְרָאתִיךָ״, וּפֵרוּשׁוֹ לְשׁוֹן גֹּבַהּ:


**to Azal** To the height of the mountaintops that are from either side. So did Menahem classify this (Machbereth p. 32), with (Isa. 41:9) “and from its nobles I called you.” Its meaning is an expression of height.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:4)


**וְנַסְתֶּם.** בִּרְאוֹתְכֶם הַמּוֹפֵת הַגָּדוֹל הַזֶּה:


**and you shall flee** when you see this great wonder.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:5)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר נָסְתַּם מִפְּנֵי הָרַעַשׁ.** (יְשַׁעְיָהוּ ו:ד) ״וַיָּנֻעוּ אַמּוֹת הַסִּפִּים״ בְּיוֹם שֶׁנִּתְנַגַּע עֻזִּיָּהוּ:


**as you fled because of the earthquake** [Isa. 6:4] “And the doorposts quaked” on the day that Uzziah was stricken with zaraath.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:5:6)


**כָּל קְדוֹשִׁים.** מַלְאָכִים:


**all holy ones** Angels.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:6:1)


**לֹא יִהְיֶה אוֹר יְקָרוֹת וְקִפָּאוֹן.** יוֹנָתָן תִּרְגֵּם: ״לָא יְהֵי נְהוֹרָא אֶלָּהֵין עָדֵי וְגָלִיד יְהֵי נְהוֹרָא״. **לֹא יִהְיֶה אוֹר** נֹגַהּ, כִּי **יְקָרוֹת וְקִפָּאוֹן** יִהְיֶה. **יְקָרוֹת** – לְשׁוֹן ״כִּיקַר כָּרִים״ (תהלים לז:כ): הוּא כְּמִין אוֹר הַנִּרְאֶה שַׁחֲרִית עַל הֶהָרִים, וְכָלֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ:


**there shall be no light, only disappearing light and thick darkness** Jonathan renders. There shall not be light, only light that passes away, and thickness, i.e., there shall not be splendrous light, only יְקָרוֹת and קִפָּאוֹן shall [there] be. יְקָרוֹת An expression similar to (Ps. 37:20), “like the disappearing light over the plains.” Like a sort of light that appears in the morning over the mountains and disappears little by little.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:6:2)


**וְקִפָּאוֹן.** קָפוּי וְחָשׁוּךְ וְעָב כַּקֶּרַח שֶׁנִּקְפָּה וּמַגְלִיד, כְּמוֹ ״וְכַגְּבִינָה תַּקְפִּיאֵנִי״ (איוב י:י):


וְקִפָּאוֹן Congealed and dark and thick, like ice that is congealed and frozen, as in (Job 10: 10), “And like cheese You curdled me.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:1)


**וְהָיָה יוֹם אֶחָד.** וְהָיָה הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה יוֹם אֶחָד מִיּוֹמוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, הוּא הַיּוֹם יָדוּעַ שֶׁהוּא לַהֲכָנַת יִשְׁעוֹ שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא (פרקי דרבי אליעזר כח):


**And it shall be one day** And this thing shall be one day of the day of the Holy One, blessed be He. That day shall be known, that it is for the preparation of the salvation by the Holy One, blessed be He.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:2)


**לֹא יוֹם וְלֹא לָיְלָה.** לֹא אוֹר נֹגַהּ כְּאוֹר שֶׁל עוֹלָם הַבָּא, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: ״וְאוֹר הַחַמָּה יִהְיֶה שִׁבְעָתַיִם כְּאוֹר וְגוֹמֵר״ (ישעיהו ל:כו):


**neither day** Neither [will it be] a light of splendor, like the light of the world to come, as it is stated (Isa. 30:26): “The light of the sun shall be sevenfold as the light of the seven days.”


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:3)


**וְלֹא לָיְלָה.** וְלֹא עֵת צָרָה כְּצָרַת שִׁעְבּוּד גָּלֻיּוֹת שֶׁמִּקֹּדֶם, שֶׁיְּמֵי מְשִׁיחֵנוּ יִהְיוּ וְאֵין בָּהֶם שִׁעְבּוּד:


**nor night** Nor a time of trouble, like the trouble of the preceding subjugation by the kingdoms, shall [these days] be, for they will be the days of the Messiah, and there shall be no subjugation during these days.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:7:4)


**וְהָיָה לְעֵת עֶרֶב.** קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּגָּמְרוּ אֶלֶף שָׁנָה יִהְיֶה אוֹר הַנֹּגַהּ וְכָל הַטּוֹב הָאָמוּר עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל (בראשית רבה ע:סב). וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לָא כִּנְהוֹר יוֹמָא וְלָא כִּקְבַל לֵילְיָא״:


**and it shall come to pass that at eventide** Before the thousand years are up there shall be a splendrous light, and all the good promised to Israel [will come]. And so did Jonathan translate: Not like the light of day and not like the darkness of night.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:8:1)


**וְהָיָה בַּיּוֹם הַהוּא.** כְּשֶׁיִּבָּקַע הַר הַזֵּיתִים מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב (זכריה יד:ד):


**And it shall come to pass on that day** when the Mount of Olives will split from east to west.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:8:2)


**יֵצְאוּ מַיִם חַיִּים מִירוּשָׁלַיִם.** וְיֵלְכוּ לַמִּזְרָח דֶּרֶךְ אוֹתָהּ בְּקִיעָה עַד **הַיָּם הַקַּדְמוֹנִי** שֶׁהוּא לְמִזְרָחוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם קֵדְמָה. וְחֶצְיוֹ יִפְנֶה לַמַּעֲרָב אֶל יָם הַמַּעֲרָבִי. הוּא הַמַּעְיָן שֶׁנִּבָּא יוֹאֵל (יואל ד:יח) ״וּמַעְיָן מִבֵּית ה׳ יֵצֵא״, הוּא הַנַּחַל שֶׁנִּבָּא (יחזקאל מז:ד) ״וַיָּמָד אֶלֶף וַיַּעֲבִרֵנִי בַמַּיִם וְגוֹ׳״:


**spring water shall come forth from Jerusalem** and flow to the east by way of the crevice to... **the eastern sea** which is to the east of the world; and half of it will turn to the west, to the western sea. This is the spring concerning which Joel (4:18) prophesied: “And a spring shall emanate from the house of the Lord.” That is the spring concerning which Ezekiel (47:3) prophesied: “And he measured a thousand cubits and he made me pass through the water.”



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:9:1)


**יִהְיֶה ה׳ אֶחָד.** שֶׁכָּל הָאֻמּוֹת יַעַזְבוּ הַבְלֵיהֶם וְיוֹדוּ לוֹ שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד וְאֵין עִמּוֹ אֵל נֵכָר:


**shall the Lord be one** For all the nations shall abandon their vanities and acknowledge Him, that He is one, and [that] no strange deity is with Him.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:9:2)


**וּשְׁמוֹ אֶחָד.** שֶׁיְּהֵא שְׁמוֹ נִזְכָּר בְּפִי כֻּלָּם:


**and His name one** That His name shall be mentioned by everyone.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:1)


**יִסּוֹב כָּל הָאָרֶץ.** יֵהָפֵךְ לִהְיוֹת כָּל הָאָרֶץ כַּעֲרָבָה, יִשְׁפְּלוּ הֶהָרִים וִיהִי כָּל הָעוֹלָם כֻּלּוֹ מִישׁוֹר (ישעיהו מ:ד), וִירוּשָׁלַיִם הַר כְּדֵי שֶׁתֵּרָאֶה גָּבוֹהַּ עַל הַכֹּל:


**The whole earth shall be changed** The whole earth shall be changed to be like a plain. The mountains will be lowered, and the whole world will be a plain; and Jerusalem will be a mountain, so that it should appear higher than everything [else in the world].


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:2)


**מִגֶּבַע לְרִמּוֹן נֶגֶב יְרוּשָׁלִָם.** שָׁנִינוּ בְּתוֹסֶפְתָּא דְּסוֹטָה (תוספתא סוטה יא,יד): נֶגֶב יְרוּשָׁלִַם מִישׁוֹר הוּא, וְגֶבַע רִמּוֹן טְרָשִׁין וְקַרְקְשִׁין. וְכֵן פִּתְרוֹנוֹ: **מִגֶּבַע לְרִמּוֹן**, שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם הָרִים, מִשָּׁם יַתְחִילוּ לִהְיוֹת נֶהְפָּכִין לַעֲרָבָה וּלְמִישׁוֹר, וְיִהְיוּ דוֹמִין לְנֶגֶב **יְרוּשָׁלִַם**, שֶׁהוּא מִישׁוֹר:


**from the hill of Rimmon** We learned in Tosefta of Sotah (11:14): South of Jerusalem is a plain, and the hill of Rimmon is rocks and clods. Rather, so is [the] interpretation [of this phrase]: From the hill of Rimmon, which is a mountainous place from there [the mountains of the world] will begin to be changed, to [be] a plain; and they will be like the south of Jerusalem, which is a plain.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:3)


**וְרָאֲמָה.** מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁכָּל סְבִיבָהּ מִישׁוֹר, תֵּרָאֶה הִיא גְּבוֹהָָָה:


**but it will be elevated high** Since its entire environs are a plain, it will appear high.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:4)


**וְיָָָשְׁבָה תַּחְתֶּיהָ.** בִּמְקוֹמָהּ:


**and remain in its old place** in its place


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:10:5)


**עַד שַׁעַר הַפִּנִּים וּמִגְדַּל חֲנַנְאֵל.** גַּם הוּא בִּמְקוֹמוֹ, וּמִשָּׁם יִמָּשֵׁךְ אֹרֶךְ הָעִיר **עַד יִקְבֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ.** תַּרְגּוּם יוֹנָתָן: ״שִׁיחֵי דְמַלְכָּא״, פושיי״ן בְּלַעַ״ז. וּכְמוֹ (בבא קמא נ:) שִׁיחִין וּמְעָרוֹת. וְכֵן כָּל יְקָבִים שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן שִׁיחִין הֵם: בּוֹר שֶׁלִּפְנֵי הַגַּת שֶׁהַיַּיִן זָב לְתוֹכוֹ. וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדָה: יִקְבֵי מֶלֶךְ יָם אוֹקְיָנוֹס, שֶׁתַּגִּיעַ יְרוּשָׁלַיִם עַד סוֹף כָּל הָעוֹלָם. יְקָבִים שֶׁחֲצָבָם מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים:


**until the corner gate, and from the tower of Hananel** which will also be in its place; and from there shall extend the length of the city **until the king’s wine-cellars** Jonathan renders: the pits off the king, fosec in Old French. [This is] like [a word in] Baba Kamma 50b, “trenches and caves.” And so, all wine cellars in Scripture are expressions of trenches, referring to the pit that is before the wine press, into which the wine flows. And the Midrash Aggadah (Pesikta d’Rav Kahana p. 143a; Song Rabbah 7:4, cf. Mattenoth Kehunnah, Radal) [identifies] the pits of the king with the ocean: that Jerusalem will reach the end of the whole world, the pits dug out by the supreme King of kings.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:11:1)


**וְחֵרֶם לֹא יִהְיֶה.** הָעִיר עוֹד:


**and there shall be no more destruction** The city shall no longer be in ruins.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:12:1)


**הָמֵק בְּשָׂרוֹ וְהוּא עוֹמֵד.** זֶה נִפּוּל אֵיבָרִים:


**his flesh will waste away while he still stands** This denotes the falling off of the limbs.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:13:1)


**תִּהְיֶה מְהוּמַת ה׳ רַבָּה בָּהֶם.** לְעַרְבֵּב חָכְמָתָם לְהָפְכָם לְשִׁגָּעוֹן:


**there will be great consternation, sent by the Lord upon them** to confuse their wisdom, to bring them about to madness.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:13:2)


**וְהֶחֱזִיקוּ אִישׁ יַד רֵעֵהוּ.** יֹאחֵז בּוֹ לְהָרְגוֹ:


**each one will seize the hand of the other** He will seize him to slay him.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:13:3)


**וְעָלְתָה יָדוֹ.** כָּל דְּאַלִּים גָּבַר (בבא בתרא לד:):


**and his hand shall rise up** Whoever is stronger is victorious.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:14:1)


**וְגַם יְהוּדָה.** עַל כָּרְחָהּ תִּלָּחֵם עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל:


**Yea, even Judah** against their will shall fight against Jerusalem.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:15:1)


**כַּמַּגֵּפָה הַזֹּאת.** הַמְפֹרֶשֶׁת לְמַעְלָה **הָמֵק בְּשָׂרוֹ**:


**similar to this plague** which is delineated above, that his flesh shall waste away.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:16:1)


**אֶת חַג הַסֻּכּוֹת.** כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה בְּמַסֶּכֶת עֲבוֹדָה זָרָה: מִצְוָה קַלָּה יֵשׁ לִי וְסֻכָּה שְׁמָהּ, כִּדְאִיתָא בִּשְׁמַעְתָּא רִאשׁוֹנָה (עבודה זרה ג.):


**the festival of Tabernacle** As our Sages explained in tractate Avodah Zarah (3a): I have an easy commandment named Sukkah, as is stated in the first halachic discussion.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:17:1)


**הַגָּשֶׁם.** גְּשָׁמִים מַמָּשׁ, לְהַצְמִיחַ פֵּרוֹת אַרְצָם. וּמָה רָאָה לִגְזֹר עֲלֵיהֶם עֲצִירַת גְּשָׁמִים? לְפִי שֶׁמִּצְוֹת הֶחָג עַל הַגְּשָׁמִים הֵם – אַרְבַּעַת מִינִים שֶׁבַּלּוּלָב וְנִסּוּךְ הַמַּיִם – לְרַצּוֹת עַל הַמַּיִם, עַל שֶׁהֶחָג זְמַן גִּשְׁמֵי שָׁנָה הוּא. וְאֵלּוּ הַמְפַקְפְּקִין בְּחַג הַסֻּכּוֹת, **לֹא עֲלֵיהֶם יִהְיֶה הַגָּשֶׁם.** כָּךְ לָמַדְתִּי בְּתוֹסֶפְתָּא דְּסֻכָּה (תוספתא סוכה ג:יח):


**rain** Actual rain to cause the produce of their land to grow. Now, what reason did He have for decreeing upon them the withholding of rain? That the commandments of the festival are based on the rains: the four species of the lulav and the water libation are to appease God for water. Since the festival of Tabernacles is the time of the rains of the year, no rain will fall on those who entertain doubts concerning the festival of Tabernacles. This is learned in the Tosefta of Sukkah (4:7).



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:1)


**וְאִם מִשְׁפַּחַת מִצְרַיִם לֹא תַעֲלֶה.** וְהֵן אֵין צְרִיכִים לִגְשָׁמִים, שֶׁנִּילוּס עוֹלֶה וּמַשְׁקֶה אוֹתָם:


**And if the family of Egypt does not go up** and they do not require rain, for the Nile comes up and waters it [i.e., the land of Egypt].


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:2)


**וְלֹא עֲלֵיהֶם.** תִּהְיֶה הַגֶּשֶׁם שֶׁלָּהֶם, כְּלוֹמַר: לֹא יַשְׁקֶה אוֹתָם נִילוּס. וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: ״לָא עֲלֵיהוֹן יִסַּק נִילוּס״:


**it shall not [rain] upon them** Their rain shall not be upon them; i.e., the Nile will not water them, and so did Jonathan render: The Nile shall not ascend upon them.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:3)


**תִּהְיֶה הַמַּגֵּפָה.** שֶׁל רָעָב:


**the plague will be** The plague of famine.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:18:4)


**אֲשֶׁר יִגֹּף ה׳‏.** זֹאת תִּהְיֶה עֹנֶשׁ מִצְרַיִם וְעֹנֶשׁ כָּל הָעוֹבְדֵי כּוֹכָָבִים וּמַזָָָָּלוֹת, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לֹא עֲלֵיהֶם יִהְיֶה הַגֶּשֶׁם:


**with which the Lord will plague** This will be the punishment of Egypt and the punishment of all the nations, as He states. “upon them there shall be no rain.”



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:20:1)


**מְצִלּוֹת הַסּוּס.** עַל הַנְּקִישׁוֹת שֶׁתּוֹלִין לַסּוּס לְנוֹי בֵּין עֵינָיו (פסחים נ.), אַף אוֹתָן יַקְדִּישׁוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת כְּלֵי שָׁרֵת לַמִּזְרָקִים לַדָּם וְלַסִּירוֹת לְבַשֵּׁל בְּשַׂר זְבָחִים לָרֹב:


**there will be upon the bells of the horses** On the bells that are hung on the horse for beauty between its eyes (Pesachim 50a). Those, too, will be consecrated to make service vessels: sprinkling basins for the blood and pots to cook the flesh of the many sacrifices.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:20:2)


**וְהָיָה הַסִּירוֹת.** שֶׁהֵם הָיוּ לְדַשֵּׁן (אֶת הַדֶּשֶׁן) אַף הֵם יִהְיוּ שֶׁל זָהָב וְשֶׁל כֶּסֶף כַּמִּזְרָקִים אֲשֶׁר לִפְנֵי הַמִּזְבֵּחַ:


**Yea, every pot...will be** All those that are used to remove the ashes, they too will be of gold and of silver, like the sprinkling basins that are before the altar.


###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:20:3)


**מְצִלּוֹת הַסּוּס.** טַנְטִינִי״ר בְּלַעַ״ז:


**the bells of the horses** tentinonc in Old French.



### Verse 21

###### Rashi on Zechariah 14:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Zechariah/r/14:21:1)


**וְלֹא יִהְיֶה כְנַעֲנִי.** לֹא יִצְטָרְכוּ לִסְחוֹר, כְּמוֹ (ישעיהו כג:ח) ״כִּנְעָנֶיהָ נִכְבַּדֵּי אָרֶץ״. דָּבָר אַחֵר: אֵין כָּאן עָנִי:


**and there will no longer be a trafficker** They will not require trafficking, as in (Isa. 23:8): “whose traffickers were the honored of the earth.” Another explanation: There is no poor man here.
